{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX","sessionId":"7950de6a-373b-44cb-ac45-a9b755daa3bc","version":"2.1.12","gitBranch":"feature/smart-window-management","agentId":"a6b0e92","slug":"reactive-snacking-metcalfe","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"Design a DYNAMIC LAYOUT ALGORITHM for the Matrix Terminal window management system that intelligently accommodates user actions.\n\n**Context:** Current system has static layout modes (Pillars, Quads) that rigidly position windows. When a user drags a window to another monitor, it snaps back. User wants a SMART system that:\n- Accommodates dragged windows (makes room for them)\n- Bumps least-used windows when at capacity\n- Remembers user-customized positions\n\n**Architecture Challenge:**\n\n1. **Dynamic Accommodation Algorithm**\n   Design algorithm for: \"User drags Window-3 from Monitor-A to Monitor-B which already has 4 windows\"\n   - How to detect the drag intention vs accidental movement\n   - How to determine which window on Monitor-B gets bumped to Monitor-A\n   - How to recalculate positions for all affected windows\n   - How to handle the transition smoothly\n\n2. **Window Usage Tracking**\n   Design system to track \"least used\" for bumping decisions:\n   - Last focused timestamp\n   - Focus duration history\n   - User-designated \"priority\" windows that never get bumped\n   - Decay algorithm for usage scores\n\n3. **Custom Snapback Positions**\n   Design system for user-defined positions:\n   - How user says \"save current positions as my snapback\"\n   - Per-monitor vs global snapback sets\n   - Named position presets (e.g., \"coding\", \"monitoring\", \"presentation\")\n\n4. **Layout Mode Integration**\n   How dynamic accommodation works with existing modes:\n   - Pillars mode: How to accommodate a new window (make columns narrower? bump one?)\n   - Quads mode: How to handle 5th window on a 4-quad screen\n   - Auto mode: Intelligent switching between modes based on window count\n\n5. **State Machine**\n   Design state transitions:\n   - Idle → Drag Detected → Calculating New Layout → Animating → New Idle\n   - What triggers each transition\n   - Rollback if user cancels drag\n\n**Codebase:** C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX\n**Key file:** WindowLayoutEngine.ps1 (current layout engine)\n\n**Deliverable:** Complete algorithm specification with pseudocode, state diagrams, and data structure definitions."},"uuid":"424e6e99-2dd7-48ea-8e8c-dad5a3b408ff","timestamp":"2026-01-19T19:36:31.222Z"}

Patterns & Conventions Found\n     4→\n     5→### Existing Window Management Patterns\n     6→\n     7→**Current Position-MatrixWindows implementations:**\n     8→- `matrix_control.ps1:349` - Control panel positioning\n     9→- `matrix_setup.ps1:284` - Setup wizard positioning\n    10→- `bluepill.ps1:172` - Blue Pill path positioning\n    11→\n    12→**Key Abstractions:**\n    13→- Windows API interop via P/Invoke (`SetWindowPos`, `EnumWindows`)\n    14→- Window handle tracking in `$global:matrixWindowHandles` hash table\n    15→- JSON state persistence in `matrix_state.json`\n    16→- Screen bounds via `[System.Windows.Forms.Screen]`\n    17→\n    18→**Established Conventions:**\n    19→```powershell\n    20→# Window handle storage pattern\n    21→$global:matrixWindowHandles = @{\n    22→    \"Matrix-1\" = @{ Handle = 123456; ProcessId = 789 }\n    23→}\n    24→\n    25→# Atomic file writes (US-001 pattern)\n    26→$temp = [System.IO.Path]::GetTempFileName()\n    27→$content | Out-File -FilePath $temp -Encoding UTF8\n    28→Move-Item -Path $temp -Destination $target -Force\n    29→\n    30→# JSON error handling (US-002 pattern)\n    31→try {\n    32→    $state = Get-Content -Path \"matrix_state.json\" | ConvertFrom-Json\n    33→} catch {\n    34→    Write-Host \"Error loading state: $_\"\n    35→    $state = @{ windows = @() }\n    36→}\n    37→```\n    38→\n    39→## 2. Architecture Decision\n    40→\n    41→**Chosen Approach: Centralized Layout Engine with Strategy Pattern**\n    42→\n    43→**Rationale:**\n    44→1. **Single Source of Truth** - One `WindowLayoutEngine` module handles all positioning logic\n    45→2. **Strategy Pattern** - Pluggable layout algorithms (Pillars, Quads, future: Cascade, Fullscreen)\n    46→3. **Live Reactivity** - Polling-based window detection triggers automatic re-layout\n    47→4. **Multi-Monitor First** - Treat single screen as special case of multi-monitor\n    48→5. **Configuration-Driven** - Layout mode stored in state, user-switchable via hotkey\n    49→\n    50→**Trade-offs:**\n    51→- **Chosen:** Complexity upfront for long-term maintainability\n    52→- **Rejected:** Quick fixes to existing code (would accumulate tech debt)\n    53→- **Chosen:** Polling for window changes (simple, reliable)\n    54→- **Rejected:** Event-driven (complex Windows API hooks)\n    55→\n    56→## 3. Component Design\n    57→\n    58→### 3.1 WindowLayoutEngine.ps1 (NEW)\n    59→\n    60→**File Path:** `C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX\\WindowLayoutEngine.ps1`\n    61→\n    62→**Responsibilities:**\n    63→- Calculate screen topology (detect monitors, working areas)\n    64→- Distribute windows across screens using chosen strategy\n    65→- Execute layout by calling Windows API\n    66→- Persist layout preferences to state\n    67→\n    68→**Dependencies:**\n    69→- `System.Windows.Forms` assembly for screen detection\n    70→- Windows API P/Invoke functions (imports from existing code)\n    71→- `matrix_state.json` for configuration\n    72→\n    73→**Public Interface:**\n    74→```powershell\n    75→# Main entry point - calculates and applies layout\n    76→function Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout {\n    77→    param(\n    78→        [Parameter(Mandatory)][hashtable]$WindowHandles,  # Key = shader name, Value = @{Handle, ProcessId}\n    79→        [ValidateSet('Pillars','Quads','Auto')][string]$Mode = 'Auto',\n    80→        [switch]$DryRun  # Calculate only, don't move windows\n    81→    )\n    82→}\n    83→\n    84→# Get current layout configuration\n    85→function Get-MatrixLayoutConfig {\n    86→    # Returns: @{ Mode = 'Pillars'; MaxPillars = 4; GapSize = 60; PreferredScreen = 0 }\n    87→}\n    88→\n    89→# Save layout configuration\n    90→function Set-MatrixLayoutConfig {\n    91→    param([hashtable]$Config)\n    92→}\n    93→\n    94→# Calculate layout without applying\n    95→function Get-MatrixWindowLayout {\n    96→    param(\n    97→        [int]$WindowCount,\n    98→        [ValidateSet('Pillars','Quads')][string]$Mode,\n    99→        [array]$Screens  # Optional: provide screen array, else auto-detect\n   100→    )\n   101→    # Returns: Array of @{ X, Y, Width, Height, ScreenIndex }\n   102→}\n   103→```\n   104→\n   105→### 3.2 Layout Strategy Functions (INTERNAL)\n   106→\n   107→```powershell\n   108→# Pillars layout: vertical columns side-by-side\n   109→function Get-PillarsLayout {\n   110→    param(\n   111→        [int]$WindowCount,\n   112→        [array]$Screens,\n   113→        [int]$MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4,\n   114→        [int]$GapSize = 60\n   115→    )\n   116→    # Returns: Array of window rectangles\n   117→}\n   118→\n   119→# Quads layout: 2x2 grid with plus-gap\n   120→function Get-QuadsLayout {\n   121→    param(\n   122→        [int]$WindowCount,\n   123→        [array]$Screens,\n   124→        [int]$GapSize = 60\n   125→    )\n   126→    # Returns: Array of window rectangles\n   127→}\n   128→\n   129→# Distribute N windows across M screens\n   130→function Get-WindowDistribution {\n   131→    param(\n   132→        [int]$WindowCount,\n   133→        [int]$ScreenCount,\n   134→        [int]$MaxPerScreen = 4\n   135→    )\n   136→    # Returns: Array of counts per screen, e.g. @(4, 4, 2) for 10 windows on 3 screens\n   137→}\n   138→```\n   139→\n   140→### 3.3 Screen Topology Detection\n   141→\n   142→```powershell\n   143→# Get all screens with working areas\n   144→function Get-ScreenTopology {\n   145→    # Returns: Array of @{ Index, Left, Top, Width, Height, IsPrimary }\n   146→}\n   147→```\n   148→\n   149→### 3.4 Integration Points\n   150→\n   151→**matrix_control.ps1 modifications:**\n   152→- Import `WindowLayoutEngine.ps1` at top\n   153→- Replace `Position-MatrixWindows` function with call to `Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout`\n   154→- Add hotkey to cycle layout modes (L: Pillars → Quads → Auto)\n   155→- Trigger re-layout on window count changes\n   156→\n   157→**matrix_setup.ps1 modifications:**\n   158→- Import `WindowLayoutEngine.ps1`\n   159→- Replace inline positioning logic with `Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout`\n   160→\n   161→**bluepill.ps1 modifications:**\n   162→- Same as matrix_setup.ps1\n   163→\n   164→## 4. Implementation Map\n   165→\n   166→### 4.1 Files to CREATE\n   167→\n   168→#### C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX\\WindowLayoutEngine.ps1\n   169→```\n   170→# Complete layout engine module\n   171→# Lines 1-50: Windows API P/Invoke declarations (SetWindowPos, etc)\n   172→# Lines 51-100: Get-ScreenTopology function\n   173→# Lines 101-150: Get-WindowDistribution function\n   174→# Lines 151-250: Get-PillarsLayout function (handles row overflow)\n   175→# Lines 251-350: Get-QuadsLayout function (handles plus-gap)\n   176→# Lines 351-400: Get-MatrixWindowLayout function (strategy selector)\n   177→# Lines 401-450: Get-MatrixLayoutConfig / Set-MatrixLayoutConfig\n   178→# Lines 451-500: Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout (main entry point)\n   179→```\n   180→\n   181→### 4.2 Files to MODIFY\n   182→\n   183→#### matrix_control.ps1\n   184→\n   185→**Line 1-30 (Imports section):**\n   186→```powershell\n   187→# ADD after existing Add-Type statements\n   188→. \"$PSScriptRoot\\WindowLayoutEngine.ps1\"\n   189→```\n   190→\n   191→**Line 349 (Position-MatrixWindows function):**\n   192→```powershell\n   193→# REPLACE entire function with:\n   194→function Position-MatrixWindows {\n   195→    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n   196→    Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowHandles $global:matrixWindowHandles -Mode $config.Mode\n   197→}\n   198→```\n   199→\n   200→**Line 600-650 (Main control loop - key handlers):**\n   201→```powershell\n   202→# ADD new hotkey handler after other key handlers\n   203→'L' {\n   204→    # Cycle layout mode: Pillars → Quads → Pillars\n   205→    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n   206→    $config.Mode = if ($config.Mode -eq 'Pillars') { 'Quads' } else { 'Pillars' }\n   207→    Set-MatrixLayoutConfig -Config $config\n   208→    Position-MatrixWindows\n   209→    $global:statusMessage = \"Layout: $($config.Mode)\"\n   210→}\n   211→```\n   212→\n   213→#### matrix_setup.ps1\n   214→\n   215→**Line 1-30:**\n   216→```powershell\n   217→# ADD import\n   218→. \"$PSScriptRoot\\WindowLayoutEngine.ps1\"\n   219→```\n   220→\n   221→**Line 284 (Position-MatrixWindows function):**\n   222→```powershell\n   223→# REPLACE entire function with call to engine\n   224→```\n   225→\n   226→#### bluepill.ps1\n   227→\n   228→**Same changes as matrix_setup.ps1**\n   229→\n   230→#### matrix_state.json\n   231→\n   232→**ADD new section:**\n   233→```json\n   234→{\n   235→  \"windows\": [ /* existing */ ],\n   236→  \"layout\": {\n   237→    \"mode\": \"Pillars\",\n   238→    \"maxPillarsPerScreen\": 4,\n   239→    \"gapSize\": 60,\n   240→    \"preferredScreen\": 0\n   241→  }\n   242→}\n   243→```\n   244→\n   245→## 5. Data Flow\n   246→\n   247→```\n   248→User Opens/Closes Window\n   249→         ↓\n   250→matrix_control.ps1: Update-MatrixWindowHandles (polling)\n   251→         ↓\n   252→Detects count change → Triggers Position-MatrixWindows\n   253→         ↓\n   254→Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout\n   255→         ↓\n   256→    ┌────┴────┐\n   257→    ↓         ↓\n   258→Get-ScreenTopology    Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n   259→    ↓                      ↓\n   260→    └──────┬───────────────┘\n   261→           ↓\n   262→    Get-MatrixWindowLayout (strategy selector)\n   263→           ↓\n   264→    ┌──────┴──────┐\n   265→    ↓             ↓\n   266→Get-PillarsLayout  Get-QuadsLayout\n   267→    ↓             ↓\n   268→    └──────┬──────┘\n   269→           ↓\n   270→    Get-WindowDistribution (distribute across screens)\n   271→           ↓\n   272→    Calculate individual rectangles (X, Y, Width, Height)\n   273→           ↓\n   274→    SetWindowPos for each window handle\n   275→           ↓\n   276→    Windows move to new positions\n   277→```\n   278→\n   279→## 6. $screenIdx -lt $Screens.Count; $screenIdx++) {\n   360→        $screen = $Screens[$screenIdx]\n   361→        $windowsOnScreen = $distribution[$screenIdx]\n   362→\n   363→        if ($windowsOnScreen -eq 0) { continue }\n   364→\n   365→        # Step 3: Determine if single row or multi-row\n   366→        if ($windowsOnScreen -le $MaxPillarsPerScreen) {\n   367→            $columns = $windowsOnScreen\n   368→            $rows = 1\n   369→        } else {\n   370→            $columns = $MaxPillarsPerScreen\n   371→            $rows = [Math]::Ceiling($windowsOnScreen / $columns)\n   372→        }\n   373→\n   374→        # Step 4: Calculate cell dimensions\n   375→        $totalHGaps = ($columns + 1) * $GapSize\n   376→        $totalVGaps = ($rows + 1) * $GapSize\n   377→        $cellWidth = [int](($screen.Width - $totalHGaps) / $columns)\n   378→        $cellHeight = [int](($screen.Height - $totalVGaps) / $rows)\n   379→\n   380→        # Step 5: Place each window in grid\n   381→        for ($i = 0; $i -lt $windowsOnScreen; $i++) {\n   382→            $col = $i % $columns\n   383→            $row = [Math]::Floor($i / $columns)\n   384→\n   385→            $x = $screen.Left + $GapSize + ($col * ($cellWidth + $GapSize))\n   386→            $y = $screen.Top + $GapSize + ($row * ($cellHeight + $GapSize))\n   387→\n   388→            $rectangles += @{\n   389→                X = $x\n   390→                Y = $y\n   391→                Width = $cellWidth\n   392→                Height = $cellHeight\n   393→                ScreenIndex = $screenIdx\n   394→                WindowIndex = $windowIndex++\n   395→            }\n   396→        }\n   397→    }\n   398→\n   399→    return $rectangles\n   400→}\n   401→```\n   402→\n   403→### 7.2 Quads Layout Algorithm\n   404→\n   405→```powershell\n   406→function Get-QuadsLayout {\n   407→    param(\n   408→        [int]$WindowCount,\n   409→        [array]$Screens,\n   410→        [int]$GapSize = 60\n   411→    )\n   412→\n   413→    $rectangles = @()\n   414→    $windowIndex = 0\n   415→    $windowsPerQuad = 4\n   416→\n   417→    # Calculate how many quads needed\n   418→    $quadsNeeded = [Math]::Ceiling($WindowCount / $windowsPerQuad)\n   419→    $quadsPerScreen = [Math]::Ceiling($quadsNeeded / $Screens.Count)\n   420→\n   421→    for ($screenIdx = 0; $screenIdx -lt $Screens.Count; $screenIdx++) {\n   422→        $screen = $Screens[$screenIdx]\n   423→\n   424→        # Plus-gap: horizontal gap in middle, vertical gap in middle\n   425→        $halfWidth = [int](($screen.Width - (3 * $GapSize)) / 2)\n   426→        $halfHeight = [int](($screen.Height - (3 * $GapSize)) / 2)\n   427→\n   428→        # Positions: TL, TR, BL, BR\n   429→        $quadPositions = @(\n   430→            @{ X = $screen.Left + $GapSize; Y = $screen.Top + $GapSize },\n   431→            @{ X = $screen.Left + (2*$GapSize) + $halfWidth; Y = $screen.Top + $GapSize },\n   432→            @{ X = $screen.Left + $GapSize; Y = $screen.Top + (2*$GapSize) + $halfHeight },\n   433→            @{ X = $screen.Left + (2*$GapSize) + $halfWidth; Y = $screen.Top + (2*$GapSize) + $halfHeight }\n   434→        )\n   435→\n   436→        for ($posIdx = 0; $posIdx -lt 4; $posIdx++) {\n   437→            if ($windowIndex -ge $WindowCount) { break }\n   438→\n   439→            $rectangles += @{\n   440→                X = $quadPositions[$posIdx].X\n   441→                Y = $quadPositions[$posIdx].Y\n   442→                Width = $halfWidth\n   443→                Height = $halfHeight\n   444→                ScreenIndex = $screenIdx\n   445→                WindowIndex = $windowIndex++\n   446→            }\n   447→        }\n   448→    }\n   449→\n   450→    return $rectangles\n   451→}\n   452→```\n   453→\n   454→### 7.3 Window Distribution Algorithm\n   455→\n   456→```powershell\n   457→function Get-WindowDistribution {\n   458→    param(\n   459→        [int]$WindowCount,\n   460→        [int]$ScreenCount,\n   461→        [int]$MaxPerScreen = 4\n   462→    )\n   463→\n   464→    $distribution = @(0) * $ScreenCount\n   465→    $windowsPerScreen = [Math]::Floor($WindowCount / $ScreenCount)\n   466→    $remainder = $WindowCount % $ScreenCount\n   467→\n   468→    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $ScreenCount; $i++) {\n   469→        $distribution[$i] = [Math]::Min($windowsPerScreen, $MaxPerScreen)\n   470→    }\n   471→\n   472→    # Distribute remainder\n   473→    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $remainder; $i++) {\n   474→        if ($distribution[$i] -lt $MaxPerScreen) {\n   475→            $distribution[$i]++\n   476→        }\n   477→    }\n   478→\n   479→    return $distribution\n   480→}\n   481→```\n   482→\n   483→## 8. Patterns & Conventions Found\n\n### Existing Window Management Patterns\n\n**Current Position-MatrixWindows implementations:**\n- `matrix_control.ps1:349` - Control panel positioning\n- `matrix_setup.ps1:284` - Setup wizard positioning\n- `bluepill.ps1:172` - Blue Pill path positioning\n\n**Key Abstractions:**\n- Windows API interop via P/Invoke (`SetWindowPos`, `EnumWindows`)\n- Window handle tracking in `$global:matrixWindowHandles` hash table\n- JSON state persistence in `matrix_state.json`\n- Screen bounds via `[System.Windows.Forms.Screen]`\n\n**Established Conventions:**\n```powershell\n# Window handle storage pattern\n$global:matrixWindowHandles = @{\n    \"Matrix-1\" = @{ Handle = 123456; ProcessId = 789 }\n}\n\n# Atomic file writes (US-001 pattern)\n$temp = [System.IO.Path]::GetTempFileName()\n$content | Out-File -FilePath $temp -Encoding UTF8\nMove-Item -Path $temp -Destination $target -Force\n\n# JSON error handling (US-002 pattern)\ntry {\n    $state = Get-Content -Path \"matrix_state.json\" | ConvertFrom-Json\n} catch {\n    Write-Host \"Error loading state: $_\"\n    $state = @{ windows = @() }\n}\n```\n\n## 2. Architecture Decision\n\n**Chosen Approach: Centralized Layout Engine with Strategy Pattern**\n\n**Rationale:**\n1. **Single Source of Truth** - One `WindowLayoutEngine` module handles all positioning logic\n2. **Strategy Pattern** - Pluggable layout algorithms (Pillars, Quads, future: Cascade, Fullscreen)\n3. **Live Reactivity** - Polling-based window detection triggers automatic re-layout\n4. **Multi-Monitor First** - Treat single screen as special case of multi-monitor\n5. **Configuration-Driven** - Layout mode stored in state, user-switchable via hotkey\n\n**Trade-offs:**\n- **Chosen:** Complexity upfront for long-term maintainability\n- **Rejected:** Quick fixes to existing code (would accumulate tech debt)\n- **Chosen:** Polling for window changes (simple, reliable)\n- **Rejected:** Event-driven (complex Windows API hooks)\n\n## 3. Component Design\n\n### 3.1 WindowLayoutEngine.ps1 (NEW)\n\n**File Path:** `C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX\\WindowLayoutEngine.ps1`\n\n**Responsibilities:**\n- Calculate screen topology (detect monitors, working areas)\n- Distribute windows across screens using chosen strategy\n- Execute layout by calling Windows API\n- Persist layout preferences to state\n\n**Dependencies:**\n- `System.Windows.Forms` assembly for screen detection\n- Windows API P/Invoke functions (imports from existing code)\n- `matrix_state.json` for configuration\n\n**Public Interface:**\n```powershell\n# Main entry point - calculates and applies layout\nfunction Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout {\n    param(\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)][hashtable]$WindowHandles,  # Key = shader name, Value = @{Handle, ProcessId}\n        [ValidateSet('Pillars','Quads','Auto')][string]$Mode = 'Auto',\n        [switch]$DryRun  # Calculate only, don't move windows\n    )\n}\n\n# Get current layout configuration\nfunction Get-MatrixLayoutConfig {\n    # Returns: @{ Mode = 'Pillars'; MaxPillars = 4; GapSize = 60; PreferredScreen = 0 }\n}\n\n# Save layout configuration\nfunction Set-MatrixLayoutConfig {\n    param([hashtable]$Config)\n}\n\n# Calculate layout without applying\nfunction Get-MatrixWindowLayout {\n    param(\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n        [ValidateSet('Pillars','Quads')][string]$Mode,\n        [array]$Screens  # Optional: provide screen array, else auto-detect\n    )\n    # Returns: Array of @{ X, Y, Width, Height, ScreenIndex }\n}\n```\n\n### 3.2 Layout Strategy Functions (INTERNAL)\n\n```powershell\n# Pillars layout: vertical columns side-by-side\nfunction Get-PillarsLayout {\n    param(\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n        [array]$Screens,\n        [int]$MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4,\n        [int]$GapSize = 60\n    )\n    # Returns: Array of window rectangles\n}\n\n# Quads layout: 2x2 grid with plus-gap\nfunction Get-QuadsLayout {\n    param(\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n        [array]$Screens,\n        [int]$GapSize = 60\n    )\n    # Returns: Array of window rectangles\n}\n\n# Distribute N windows across M screens\nfunction Get-WindowDistribution {\n    param(\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n        [int]$ScreenCount,\n        [int]$MaxPerScreen = 4\n    )\n    # Returns: Array of counts per screen, e.g. @(4, 4, 2) for 10 windows on 3 screens\n}\n```\n\n### 3.3 Screen Topology Detection\n\n```powershell\n# Get all screens with working areas\nfunction Get-ScreenTopology {\n    # Returns: Array of @{ Index, Left, Top, Width, Height, IsPrimary }\n}\n```\n\n### 3.4 Integration Points\n\n**matrix_control.ps1 modifications:**\n- Import `WindowLayoutEngine.ps1` at top\n- Replace `Position-MatrixWindows` function with call to `Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout`\n- Add hotkey to cycle layout modes (L: Pillars → Quads → Auto)\n- Trigger re-layout on window count changes\n\n**matrix_setup.ps1 modifications:**\n- Import `WindowLayoutEngine.ps1`\n- Replace inline positioning logic with `Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout`\n\n**bluepill.ps1 modifications:**\n- Same as matrix_setup.ps1\n\n## 4. Implementation Map\n\n### 4.1 Files to CREATE\n\n#### C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX\\WindowLayoutEngine.ps1\n```\n# Complete layout engine module\n# Lines 1-50: Windows API P/Invoke declarations (SetWindowPos, etc)\n# Lines 51-100: Get-ScreenTopology function\n# Lines 101-150: Get-WindowDistribution function\n# Lines 151-250: Get-PillarsLayout function (handles row overflow)\n# Lines 251-350: Get-QuadsLayout function (handles plus-gap)\n# Lines 351-400: Get-MatrixWindowLayout function (strategy selector)\n# Lines 401-450: Get-MatrixLayoutConfig / Set-MatrixLayoutConfig\n# Lines 451-500: Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout (main entry point)\n```\n\n### 4.2 Files to MODIFY\n\n#### matrix_control.ps1\n\n**Line 1-30 (Imports section):**\n```powershell\n# ADD after existing Add-Type statements\n. \"$PSScriptRoot\\WindowLayoutEngine.ps1\"\n```\n\n**Line 349 (Position-MatrixWindows function):**\n```powershell\n# REPLACE entire function with:\nfunction Position-MatrixWindows {\n    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n    Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowHandles $global:matrixWindowHandles -Mode $config.Mode\n}\n```\n\n**Line 600-650 (Main control loop - key handlers):**\n```powershell\n# ADD new hotkey handler after other key handlers\n'L' {\n    # Cycle layout mode: Pillars → Quads → Pillars\n    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n    $config.Mode = if ($config.Mode -eq 'Pillars') { 'Quads' } else { 'Pillars' }\n    Set-MatrixLayoutConfig -Config $config\n    Position-MatrixWindows\n    $global:statusMessage = \"Layout: $($config.Mode)\"\n}\n```\n\n#### matrix_setup.ps1\n\n**Line 1-30:**\n```powershell\n# ADD import\n. \"$PSScriptRoot\\WindowLayoutEngine.ps1\"\n```\n\n**Line 284 (Position-MatrixWindows function):**\n```powershell\n# REPLACE entire function with call to engine\n```\n\n#### bluepill.ps1\n\n**Same changes as matrix_setup.ps1**\n\n#### matrix_state.json\n\n**ADD new section:**\n```json\n{\n  \"windows\": [ /* existing */ ],\n  \"layout\": {\n    \"mode\": \"Pillars\",\n    \"maxPillarsPerScreen\": 4,\n    \"gapSize\": 60,\n    \"preferredScreen\": 0\n  }\n}\n```\n\n## 5. Data Flow\n\n```\nUser Opens/Closes Window\n         ↓\nmatrix_control.ps1: Update-MatrixWindowHandles (polling)\n         ↓\nDetects count change → Triggers Position-MatrixWindows\n         ↓\nInvoke-MatrixWindowLayout\n         ↓\n    ┌────┴────┐\n    ↓         ↓\nGet-ScreenTopology    Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n    ↓                      ↓\n    └──────┬───────────────┘\n           ↓\n    Get-MatrixWindowLayout (strategy selector)\n           ↓\n    ┌──────┴──────┐\n    ↓             ↓\nGet-PillarsLayout  Get-QuadsLayout\n    ↓             ↓\n    └──────┬──────┘\n           ↓\n    Get-WindowDistribution (distribute across screens)\n           ↓\n    Calculate individual rectangles (X, Y, Width, Height)\n           ↓\n    SetWindowPos for each window handle\n           ↓\n    Windows move to new positions\n```\n\n## 6. Build Sequence\n\n### Phase 1: Core Layout Engine (US-007 prerequisite)\n- [ ] Create `WindowLayoutEngine.ps1` file\n- [ ] Implement Windows API P/Invoke declarations (copy from existing code)\n- [ ] Implement `Get-ScreenTopology` function\n- [ ] Test screen detection on single/dual monitor setups\n- [ ] Implement `Get-WindowDistribution` algorithm\n- [ ] Write unit tests for distribution (1-10 windows, 1-3 screens)\n\n### Phase 2: Pillars Layout Algorithm\n- [ ] Implement `Get-PillarsLayout` for single screen, single row\n- [ ] Test with 1, 2, 3, 4 windows\n- [ ] Add row overflow logic (5+ Test end-to-end with real windows\n\n### Phase 6: Integration with Control Panel\n- [ ] Add import statement to `matrix_control.ps1`\n- [ ] Replace `Position-MatrixWindows` function\n- [ ] Add 'L' key 1, 2 windows\n\n### Phase 8: Edge Case Hardening\n- [ ] Handle zero windows (no-op)\n- [ ] Handle 10+ windows (graceful overflow)\n- [ ] Handle screen disconnect (re-layout to remaining screens)\n- [ ] Handle invalid window handles (skip and continue)\n- [ ] Add verbose logging (integrates with US-009)\n\n## 7. Critical Details\n\n### 7.1 Pillars Layout Algorithm\n\n```powershell\nfunction Get-PillarsLayout {\n    param(\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n        [array]$Screens,\n        [int]$MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4,\n        [int]$GapSize = 60\n    )\n\n    # Step 1: Distribute windows across screens\n    $distribution = Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount $WindowCount `\n                                          -ScreenCount $Screens.Count `\n                                          -MaxPerScreen $MaxPillarsPerScreen\n\n    $rectangles = @()\n    $windowIndex = 0\n\n    # Step 2: For each screen, layout its assigned windows\n    for ($screenIdx = 0; $screenIdx -lt $Screens.Count; $screenIdx++) {\n        $screen = $Screens[$screenIdx]\n        $windowsOnScreen = $distribution[$screenIdx]\n\n        if ($windowsOnScreen -eq 0) { continue }\n\n        # Step 3: Determine if single row or multi-row\n        if ($windowsOnScreen -le $MaxPillarsPerScreen) {\n            $columns = $windowsOnScreen\n            $rows = 1\n        } else {\n            $columns = $MaxPillarsPerScreen\n            $rows = [Math]::Ceiling($windowsOnScreen / $columns)\n        }\n\n        # Step 4: Calculate cell dimensions\n        $totalHGaps = ($columns + 1) * $GapSize\n        $totalVGaps = ($rows + 1) * $GapSize\n        $cellWidth = [int](($screen.Width - $totalHGaps) / $columns)\n        $cellHeight = [int](($screen.Height - $totalVGaps) / $rows)\n\n        # Step 5: Place each window in grid\n        for ($i = 0; $i -lt $windowsOnScreen; $i++) {\n            $col = $i % $columns\n            $row = [Math]::Floor($i / $columns)\n\n            $x = $screen.Left + $GapSize + ($col * ($cellWidth + $GapSize))\n            $y = $screen.Top + $GapSize + ($row * ($cellHeight + $GapSize))\n\n            $rectangles += @{\n                X = $x\n                Y = $y\n                Width = $cellWidth\n                Height = $cellHeight\n                ScreenIndex = $screenIdx\n                WindowIndex = $windowIndex++\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return $rectangles\n}\n```\n\n### 7.2 Quads Layout Algorithm\n\n```powershell\nfunction Get-QuadsLayout {\n    param(\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n        [array]$Screens,\n        [int]$GapSize = 60\n    )\n\n    $rectangles = @()\n    $windowIndex = 0\n    $windowsPerQuad = 4\n\n    # Calculate how many quads needed\n    $quadsNeeded = [Math]::Ceiling($WindowCount / $windowsPerQuad)\n    $quadsPerScreen = [Math]::Ceiling($quadsNeeded / $Screens.Count)\n\n    for ($screenIdx = 0; $screenIdx -lt $Screens.Count; $screenIdx++) {\n        $screen = $Screens[$screenIdx]\n\n        # Plus-gap: horizontal gap in middle, vertical gap in middle\n        $halfWidth = [int](($screen.Width - (3 * $GapSize)) / 2)\n        $halfHeight = [int](($screen.Height - (3 * $GapSize)) / 2)\n\n        # Positions: TL, TR, BL, BR\n        $quadPositions = @(\n            @{ X = $screen.Left + $GapSize; Y = $screen.Top + $GapSize },\n            @{ X = $screen.Left + (2*$GapSize) + $halfWidth; Y = $screen.Top + $GapSize },\n            @{ X = $screen.Left + $GapSize; Y = $screen.Top + (2*$GapSize) + $halfHeight },\n            @{ X = $screen.Left + (2*$GapSize) + $halfWidth; Y = $screen.Top + (2*$GapSize) + $halfHeight }\n        )\n\n        for ($posIdx = 0; $posIdx -lt 4; $posIdx++) {\n            if ($windowIndex -ge $WindowCount) { break }\n\n            $rectangles += @{\n                X = $quadPositions[$posIdx].X\n                Y = $quadPositions[$posIdx].Y\n                Width = $halfWidth\n                Height = $halfHeight\n                ScreenIndex = $screenIdx\n                WindowIndex = $windowIndex++\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return $rectangles\n}\n```\n\n### 7.3 Window Distribution Algorithm\n\n```powershell\nfunction Get-WindowDistribution {\n    param(\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n        [int]$ScreenCount,\n        [int]$MaxPerScreen = 4\n    )\n\n    $distribution = @(0) * $ScreenCount\n    $windowsPerScreen = [Math]::Floor($WindowCount / $ScreenCount)\n    $remainder = $WindowCount % $ScreenCount\n\n    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $ScreenCount; $i++) {\n        $distribution[$i] = [Math]::Min($windowsPerScreen, $MaxPerScreen)\n    }\n\n    # Distribute remainder\n    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $remainder; $i++) {\n        if ($distribution[$i] -lt $MaxPerScreen) {\n            $distribution[$i]++\n        }\n    }\n\n    return $distribution\n}\n```\n\n## 8. UI Controls for Control Panel\n\n### Hotkey: L (Layout Mode)\n- Press L to cycle: Pillars → Quads → Pillars\n- Status bar shows current mode: \"Layout: Pillars\" or \"Layout: Quads\"\n- Immediate re-layout on mode change\n\n### UI Display Update\nAdd to control panel header:\n```\n┌─ REDPILL CONTROL ─────────────────────────────┐\n│ Windows: 4  │  Layout: PILLARS  │  Screen: 1  │\n└───────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### Help Text Update\n```\n[L] Layout mode (Pillars/Quads)\n[P] Position windows\n```\n\n## 9. Next Steps\n\n1. **Implement Phase 1-2** (Core engine + Pillars layout) - provides immediate improvement\n2. **Test with real windows** - validate on 1080p single screen\n3. **Implement Phase 3** (Quads layout) - adds user choice\n4. **Implement Phase 6** (Control panel integration) - makes it interactive\n5. **Implement Phase 7** (Setup script integration) - completes rollout\n6. **Implement Phase 8** (Edge cases) - final hardening\n\nThis satisfies US-007 (robust window positioning) and provides foundation for future enhancements.\n","numLines":514,"startLine":1,"totalLines":514}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a03e6620-45eb-47ce-ab49-ea873650d765"}
