Let me create a detailed algorithm specification document for the Dynamic Layout Algorithm."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":25295,"cache_read_input_tokens":41207,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":25295,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CXHJdwycQ7DCfjhHCN255","type":"assistant","uuid":"abda2b3a-caf8-4de2-8b55-34896bb9c78b","timestamp":"2026-01-19T19:40:35.556Z"}
{"parentUuid":"abda2b3a-caf8-4de2-8b55-34896bb9c78b","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX","sessionId":"7950de6a-373b-44cb-ac45-a9b755daa3bc","version":"2.1.12","gitBranch":"feature/smart-window-management","agentId":"a6b0e92","slug":"reactive-snacking-metcalfe","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_01QtijxA97TEhTYY4De6wnHb","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_011SAswPnMyxvW7T8kjVirXp","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\Users\\ehome\\.claude\\plans\\reactive-snacking-metcalfe-agent-a6b0e92.md","content":"# Dynamic Layout Algorithm Specification\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThis document specifies a **Dynamic Layout Algorithm** for the Matrix Terminal window management system. The algorithm transforms the current static layout engine (Pillars/Quads modes with rigid positioning) into an intelligent, user-accommodating system that:\n\n1. Detects user drag intentions and accommodates window moves\n2. Intelligently bumps least-used windows when monitors reach capacity\n3. Remembers user-customized positions with named presets\n4. Smoothly integrates with existing layout modes\n\n---\n\n## 1. Architecture Overview\n\n### 1.1 Current State Analysis\n\n**Existing Components:**\n- `WindowLayoutEngine.ps1` (1520 lines) - Static layout engine with Pillars/Quads modes\n- `matrix_control.ps1` - Control panel with Shift+L layout cycling\n- `window-registry.json` - Maps window handles to shader files\n- `matrix_state.json` - Persists layout config and window positions\n\n**Current Limitations:**\n- Windows snap back to calculated positions on any re-layout trigger\n- No accommodation for user-dragged windows\n- No usage tracking for intelligent window bumping\n- Position persistence is all-or-nothing (restore entire layout or recalculate)\n\n### 1.2 Proposed Architecture\n\n```\n                                    +------------------------+\n                                    |   Dynamic Layout       |\n                                    |   Coordinator          |\n                                    +------------------------+\n                                              |\n          +-----------------------------------+-----------------------------------+\n          |                   |                   |                   |\n+-----------------+  +------------------+  +------------------+  +------------------+\n| Drag Detection  |  | Usage Tracking   |  | Position         |  | Layout Strategy  |\n| Engine          |  | System           |  | Presets Manager  |  | Adapter          |\n+-----------------+  +------------------+  +------------------+  +------------------+\n| - Movement      |  | - Focus time     |  | - Named presets  |  | - Pillars        |\n|   threshold     |  | - Last active    |  | - Per-monitor    |  | - Quads          |\n| - Cross-monitor |  | - Priority flags |  | - Global sets    |  | - Dynamic        |\n|   detection     |  | - Decay scoring  |  | - Auto-save      |  |   accommodation  |\n+-----------------+  +------------------+  +------------------+  +------------------+\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Dynamic Accommodation Algorithm\n\n### 2.1 Scenario: User Drags Window-3 from Monitor-A to Monitor-B (4 windows)\n\n**Problem Statement:**\nUser drags Window-3 from Monitor-A (which has 2 windows) to Monitor-B (which already has 4 windows at capacity).\n\n**Algorithm Steps:**\n\n```\nALGORITHM: DynamicAccommodation\n\nINPUT:\n  - draggedWindow: Window being dragged\n  - sourceMonitor: Monitor-A (original location)\n  - targetMonitor: Monitor-B (drop destination)\n  - targetCapacity: 4 (max windows per monitor)\n  - allWindows: List of all Matrix windows\n\nOUTPUT:\n  - newLayout: Updated positions for all affected windows\n  - bumpedWindow: Window moved from target to source (if any)\n\nPROCEDURE:\n  1. DETECT_DRAG_INTENTION(draggedWindow, movementData)\n     IF movementDelta < DRAG_THRESHOLD (50px)\n       RETURN null  // Accidental movement, ignore\n\n     IF dragDuration < MIN_DRAG_TIME (300ms)\n       RETURN null  // Too fast, likely unintentional\n\n     targetMonitor = GET_MONITOR_AT_POSITION(releasePosition)\n     IF targetMonitor == sourceMonitor\n       RETURN SNAP_TO_NEAREST_SLOT(draggedWindow, sourceMonitor)\n\n  2. CHECK_TARGET_CAPACITY(targetMonitor)\n     windowsOnTarget = GET_WINDOWS_ON_MONITOR(targetMonitor)\n\n     IF windowsOnTarget.Count < targetCapacity\n       // Space available - simple accommodation\n       ASSIGN_SLOT(draggedWindow, targetMonitor)\n       RECALCULATE_LAYOUT(targetMonitor)\n       RECALCULATE_LAYOUT(sourceMonitor)\n       RETURN newLayout\n\n  3. SELECT_WINDOW_TO_BUMP(windowsOnTarget, draggedWindow)\n     // Use usage scoring to find least-used window\n     candidates = windowsOnTarget.FILTER(w => !w.isPriorityLocked)\n\n     IF candidates.isEmpty\n       RETURN REJECT_DROP()  // All windows are priority-locked\n\n     bumpedWindow = candidates.MIN_BY(w => w.usageScore)\n\n  4. EXECUTE_BUMP(bumpedWindow, sourceMonitor)\n     MOVE_WINDOW(bumpedWindow, sourceMonitor)\n     MOVE_WINDOW(draggedWindow, targetMonitor)\n\n  5. RECALCULATE_LAYOUTS()\n     RECALCULATE_LAYOUT(sourceMonitor)\n     RECALCULATE_LAYOUT(targetMonitor)\n\n  6. UPDATE_TRACKING()\n     UPDATE_POSITION_CACHE(allWindows)\n     UPDATE_USAGE_SCORES(draggedWindow, BOOST)  // User actively used it\n```\n\n### 2.2 Drag Intention Detection\n\n**Criteria for Valid Drag:**\n\n| Criterion | Threshold | Rationale |\n|-----------|-----------|-----------|\n| Movement delta | >= 50px | Below this could be window resize or accidental click |\n| Drag duration | >= 300ms | Quick movements are likely accidental |\n| Cross-monitor | Center crosses boundary | Window center must be on new monitor |\n| Release position | Stable for 100ms | User paused to \"place\" the window |\n\n**Pseudocode:**\n\n```\nFUNCTION: DetectDragIntention\n\nSTATE:\n  - dragStartPosition: {x, y}\n  - dragStartTime: timestamp\n  - dragStartMonitor: MonitorIndex\n  - lastMovementTime: timestamp\n\nPROCEDURE:\n  // Called every 100ms while window is moving\n\n  currentPosition = GET_WINDOW_CENTER(window)\n  currentMonitor = GET_MONITOR_AT_POSITION(currentPosition)\n\n  movementDelta = DISTANCE(dragStartPosition, currentPosition)\n  dragDuration = NOW() - dragStartTime\n\n  // Check if this looks like intentional drag\n  IF movementDelta < 50 AND dragDuration < 500ms\n    RETURN { intention: 'none', confidence: 0.0 }\n\n  IF currentMonitor != dragStartMonitor\n    // Cross-monitor drag detected\n    timeSinceLastMove = NOW() - lastMovementTime\n\n    IF timeSinceLastMove > 100ms\n      // User has paused on new monitor - likely intentional placement\n      RETURN {\n        intention: 'cross-monitor',\n        targetMonitor: currentMonitor,\n        confidence: MIN(1.0, dragDuration / 1000ms)\n      }\n\n  // Same-monitor reposition\n  RETURN {\n    intention: 'reposition',\n    targetMonitor: currentMonitor,\n    confidence: 0.5\n  }\n```\n\n### 2.3 Bump Selection Algorithm\n\n**Criteria for Selecting Window to Bump:**\n\n1. **Priority Lock** - Windows marked as \"priority\" are never bumped\n2. **Usage Score** - Lower score = more likely to be bumped\n3. **Recency** - Windows not focused recently are candidates\n4. **Position** - Prefer bumping windows closest to the source monitor\n\n**Usage Score Calculation:**\n\n```\nFUNCTION: CalculateUsageScore(window)\n\nPARAMETERS:\n  - focusWeight = 0.4      // Weight for focus duration\n  - recencyWeight = 0.3    // Weight for last active time\n  - frequencyWeight = 0.3  // Weight for focus frequency\n\nPROCEDURE:\n  // Focus duration (normalized to 0-1)\n  totalFocusTime = window.focusHistory.SUM(session => session.duration)\n  maxFocusTime = 3600000  // 1 hour cap\n  focusScore = MIN(1.0, totalFocusTime / maxFocusTime)\n\n  // Recency (exponential decay)\n  timeSinceLastFocus = NOW() - window.lastFocusTime\n  decayHalfLife = 1800000  // 30 minutes\n  recencyScore = POW(0.5, timeSinceLastFocus / decayHalfLife)\n\n  // Frequency (focus sessions per hour)\n  sessionsLastHour = window.focusHistory.COUNT(s => s.startTime > NOW() - 3600000)\n  maxSessions = 20  // Cap at 20 sessions per hour\n  frequencyScore = MIN(1.0, sessionsLastHour / maxSessions)\n\n  // Weighted combination\n  usageScore = (focusScore * focusWeight) +\n               (recencyScore * recencyWeight) +\n               (frequencyScore * frequencyWeight)\n\n  // Priority boost (if window is priority-locked)\n  IF window.isPriorityLocked\n    usageScore = 999.0  // Effectively unbumpable\n\n  RETURN usageScore\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Window Usage Tracking System\n\n### 3.1 Data Structure\n\n```\nWindowUsageData = {\n  windowId: string,           // \"Matrix-1\", \"Matrix-2\", etc.\n\n  // Focus tracking\n  lastFocusTime: timestamp,   // Last time window received focus\n  totalFocusTime: number,     // Cumulative focus duration (ms)\n  focusHistory: [             // Rolling window of focus sessions\n    { startTime: timestamp, duration: number }\n  ],\n\n  // User preferences\n  isPriorityLocked: boolean,  // User marked as \"never bump\"\n  preferredMonitor: number,   // User's preferred monitor (-1 = no preference)\n  preferredSlot: number,      // User's preferred slot on monitor (-1 = no preference)\n\n  // Calculated scores (updated on access)\n  usageScore: number,         // 0.0 - 1.0 (higher = more used)\n  lastScoreUpdate: timestamp  // When score was last calculated\n}\n```\n\n### 3.2 Focus Tracking Implementation\n\n```\nFUNCTION: TrackWindowFocus\n\nSTATE:\n  - currentlyFocusedWindow: WindowId | null\n  - focusStartTime: timestamp | null\n\nEVENTS:\n  ON_WINDOW_FOCUS_GAINED(windowId):\n    // End previous focus session\n    IF currentlyFocusedWindow != null\n      EndFocusSession(currentlyFocusedWindow)\n\n    // Start new focus session\n    currentlyFocusedWindow = windowId\n    focusStartTime = NOW()\n\n    // Update last focus time\n    usageData[windowId].lastFocusTime = NOW()\n\n  ON_WINDOW_FOCUS_LOST(windowId):\n    IF currentlyFocusedWindow == windowId\n      EndFocusSession(windowId)\n      currentlyFocusedWindow = null\n\nFUNCTION: EndFocusSession(windowId)\n  duration = NOW() - focusStartTime\n\n  // Add to focus history (keep last 100 sessions)\n  usageData[windowId].focusHistory.PUSH({\n    startTime: focusStartTime,\n    duration: duration\n  })\n  IF usageData[windowId].focusHistory.LENGTH > 100\n    usageData[windowId].focusHistory.SHIFT()\n\n  // Update total focus time\n  usageData[windowId].totalFocusTime += duration\n```\n\n### 3.3 Score Decay Algorithm\n\nUsage scores decay over time to ensure recently-used windows are protected from bumping while idle windows become candidates.\n\n```\nFUNCTION: ApplyScoreDecay\n\nPARAMETERS:\n  - decayInterval = 60000    // Check every 60 seconds\n  - decayFactor = 0.95       // Multiply score by this each interval\n  - minimumScore = 0.1       // Floor to prevent complete decay\n\nPROCEDURE (runs on timer):\n  FOR EACH window IN usageData\n    timeSinceLastUpdate = NOW() - window.lastScoreUpdate\n    decayPeriods = FLOOR(timeSinceLastUpdate / decayInterval)\n\n    IF decayPeriods > 0\n      decayMultiplier = POW(decayFactor, decayPeriods)\n      window.usageScore = MAX(minimumScore, window.usageScore * decayMultiplier)\n      window.lastScoreUpdate = NOW()\n```\n\n### 3.4 Persistence\n\nUsage data persists in `matrix_state.json`:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"layout\": { ... },\n  \"windowPositions\": { ... },\n  \"usageTracking\": {\n    \"Matrix-1\": {\n      \"lastFocusTime\": \"2026-01-19T10:30:00.000Z\",\n      \"totalFocusTime\": 1800000,\n      \"focusHistory\": [\n        { \"startTime\": \"2026-01-19T10:00:00.000Z\", \"duration\": 600000 },\n        { \"startTime\": \"2026-01-19T10:15:00.000Z\", \"duration\": 1200000 }\n      ],\n      \"isPriorityLocked\": false,\n      \"preferredMonitor\": 0,\n      \"preferredSlot\": -1,\n      \"usageScore\": 0.75,\n      \"lastScoreUpdate\": \"2026-01-19T10:30:00.000Z\"\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Custom Snapback Positions (Presets)\n\n### 4.1 Preset Data Structure\n\n```\nPositionPreset = {\n  name: string,              // \"Coding\", \"Monitoring\", \"Presentation\"\n  createdAt: timestamp,\n  lastUsedAt: timestamp,\n\n  // Scope\n  scope: 'global' | 'per-monitor',\n  monitorConfiguration: string,  // Hash of monitor topology for validation\n\n  // Positions (keyed by window ID)\n  positions: {\n    \"Matrix-1\": { x: 60, y: 90, width: 560, height: 930, monitorIndex: 0 },\n    \"Matrix-2\": { x: 680, y: 90, width: 560, height: 930, monitorIndex: 0 },\n    ...\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 4.2 Preset Management API\n\n```\nFUNCTION: SaveCurrentAsPreset(presetName)\n\n  // Capture current window positions\n  windowInfo = Get-MatrixWindowInfo()\n  positions = {}\n\n  FOR EACH window IN windowInfo\n    rect = GET_WINDOW_RECT(window.Handle)\n    monitorIndex = GET_MONITOR_INDEX_FOR_RECT(rect)\n\n    positions[window.ShaderName] = {\n      x: rect.Left,\n      y: rect.Top,\n      width: rect.Right - rect.Left,\n      height: rect.Bottom - rect.Top,\n      monitorIndex: monitorIndex\n    }\n\n  // Generate monitor configuration hash\n  screens = Get-ScreenTopology()\n  monitorConfig = HASH(screens.MAP(s => \"${s.Width}x${s.Height}@${s.Left},${s.Top}\"))\n\n  preset = {\n    name: presetName,\n    createdAt: NOW(),\n    lastUsedAt: NOW(),\n    scope: 'global',\n    monitorConfiguration: monitorConfig,\n    positions: positions\n  }\n\n  // Save to state\n  state.positionPresets[presetName] = preset\n  SAVE_STATE(state)\n\n  RETURN preset\n\n\nFUNCTION: ApplyPreset(presetName)\n\n  preset = state.positionPresets[presetName]\n  IF preset == null\n    RETURN { success: false, error: \"Preset not found\" }\n\n  // Validate monitor configuration matches\n  currentMonitorConfig = GET_CURRENT_MONITOR_CONFIG_HASH()\n  IF preset.monitorConfiguration != currentMonitorConfig\n    // Monitor setup changed - need adaptation\n    RETURN AdaptPresetToCurrentMonitors(preset)\n\n  // Apply positions directly\n  FOR EACH windowId, position IN preset.positions\n    window = FIND_WINDOW_BY_ID(windowId)\n    IF window != null\n      SET_WINDOW_POS(window.Handle, position.x, position.y, position.width, position.height)\n\n  // Update last used time\n  preset.lastUsedAt = NOW()\n  SAVE_STATE(state)\n\n  RETURN { success: true }\n\n\nFUNCTION: AdaptPresetToCurrentMonitors(preset)\n  // Monitor topology changed - intelligently adapt positions\n\n  currentScreens = Get-ScreenTopology()\n\n  FOR EACH windowId, position IN preset.positions\n    window = FIND_WINDOW_BY_ID(windowId)\n    IF window == null\n      CONTINUE\n\n    originalMonitor = position.monitorIndex\n\n    IF originalMonitor < currentScreens.Count\n      // Target monitor still exists - scale position\n      targetScreen = currentScreens[originalMonitor]\n      scaledPosition = SCALE_POSITION_TO_SCREEN(position, targetScreen)\n      SET_WINDOW_POS(window.Handle, scaledPosition)\n    ELSE\n      // Target monitor no longer exists - place on primary\n      primaryScreen = currentScreens[0]\n      scaledPosition = SCALE_POSITION_TO_SCREEN(position, primaryScreen)\n      SET_WINDOW_POS(window.Handle, scaledPosition)\n\n  RETURN { success: true, adapted: true }\n```\n\n### 4.3 Auto-Save Behavior\n\nThe system automatically saves window positions when:\n1. User exits the control panel (ESC key)\n2. User switches layout modes (Shift+L)\n3. After drag accommodation completes\n4. On a timer (every 5 minutes if positions changed)\n\n```\nFUNCTION: SetupAutoSave\n\nSTATE:\n  - lastAutoSave: timestamp\n  - positionsAtLastSave: PositionSnapshot\n  - autoSaveInterval: 300000  // 5 minutes\n\nON_INTERVAL(autoSaveInterval):\n  currentPositions = GET_ALL_WINDOW_POSITIONS()\n\n  IF currentPositions != positionsAtLastSave\n    // Positions changed - auto-save\n    SaveToQuickSnapshot()\n    positionsAtLastSave = currentPositions\n    lastAutoSave = NOW()\n\nFUNCTION: SaveToQuickSnapshot()\n  // Save to special \"_autosave\" preset that can be restored on next session\n  SaveCurrentAsPreset(\"_autosave\")\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Layout Mode Integration\n\n### 5.1 Pillars Mode with Dynamic Accommodation\n\nWhen in Pillars mode, the system can accommodate new windows by:\n\n**Option A: Narrow Existing Columns**\n```\nSCENARIO: 4 pillars on monitor, user drags 5th window to same monitor\n\nBEFORE: [====][====][====][====]  (4 equal-width pillars)\nAFTER:  [===][===][===][===][===] (5 narrower pillars)\n\nCONSTRAINTS:\n  - Minimum pillar width: 300px\n  - If new width < minimum: trigger bump instead\n```\n\n**Option B: Bump Least-Used**\n```\nSCENARIO: 4 pillars at minimum width, user drags 5th window\n\nACTION:\n  1. Identify least-used window via usage scoring\n  2. Bump it to another monitor (or minimize if no monitors available)\n  3. Place dragged window in vacated slot\n  4. Keep pillar widths at minimum\n```\n\n**Implementation:**\n\n```\nFUNCTION: AccommodateInPillarsMode(targetMonitor, draggedWindow)\n\n  currentWindows = GET_WINDOWS_ON_MONITOR(targetMonitor)\n  newCount = currentWindows.Count + 1\n\n  // Calculate new pillar width\n  availableWidth = targetMonitor.Width - (newCount + 1) * GAP_SIZE\n  newPillarWidth = availableWidth / newCount\n\n  IF newPillarWidth >= MIN_PILLAR_WIDTH (300px)\n    // Space available - narrow all pillars\n    RETURN {\n      action: 'narrow',\n      newLayout: CALCULATE_PILLARS_LAYOUT(newCount, targetMonitor)\n    }\n  ELSE\n    // At capacity - need to bump\n    bumpCandidate = SELECT_WINDOW_TO_BUMP(currentWindows, draggedWindow)\n\n    IF bumpCandidate == null\n      // All windows priority-locked\n      RETURN { action: 'reject' }\n\n    RETURN {\n      action: 'bump',\n      bumpWindow: bumpCandidate,\n      newLayout: CALCULATE_PILLARS_LAYOUT(currentWindows.Count, targetMonitor)\n    }\n```\n\n### 5.2 Quads Mode with Dynamic Accommodation\n\nQuads mode has a natural 4-window-per-monitor limit. Accommodation options:\n\n**Option A: Convert to 3x2 Grid**\n```\nSCENARIO: 4 quads on monitor, user drags 5th window\n\nBEFORE:                      AFTER:\n┌─────┐ ┌─────┐             ┌───┐ ┌───┐ ┌───┐\n│  1  │ │  2  │             │ 1 │ │ 2 │ │ 3 │\n└─────┘ └─────┘             └───┘ └───┘ └───┘\n┌─────┐ ┌─────┐             ┌───┐ ┌───┐\n│  3  │ │  4  │             │ 4 │ │ 5 │\n└─────┘ └─────┘             └───┘ └───┘\n\n(Converts from 2x2 to 3x2 grid)\n```\n\n**Option B: Bump to Maintain 2x2**\n```\nSCENARIO: User explicitly wants 2x2 layout\n\nACTION:\n  1. Bump least-used window to another monitor\n  2. Place dragged window in vacated quad\n  3. Maintain 2x2 layout\n```\n\n**Implementation:**\n\n```\nFUNCTION: AccommodateInQuadsMode(targetMonitor, draggedWindow)\n\n  currentWindows = GET_WINDOWS_ON_MONITOR(targetMonitor)\n\n  IF currentWindows.Count < 4\n    // Space available in quad\n    RETURN {\n      action: 'place',\n      newLayout: CALCULATE_QUADS_LAYOUT(currentWindows.Count + 1, targetMonitor)\n    }\n\n  // At 4-window capacity - check user preference\n  layoutConfig = GET_LAYOUT_CONFIG()\n\n  IF layoutConfig.quadsOverflowMode == 'expand'\n    // Convert to 3x2 grid\n    RETURN {\n      action: 'expand',\n      newLayout: CALCULATE_GRID_LAYOUT(5, targetMonitor, cols=3, rows=2)\n    }\n  ELSE\n    // Bump least-used\n    bumpCandidate = SELECT_WINDOW_TO_BUMP(currentWindows, draggedWindow)\n\n    RETURN {\n      action: 'bump',\n      bumpWindow: bumpCandidate,\n      newLayout: CALCULATE_QUADS_LAYOUT(4, targetMonitor)\n    }\n```\n\n### 5.3 Auto Mode Intelligence\n\nAuto mode selects the best layout based on window count and user behavior:\n\n```\nFUNCTION: SelectAutoLayout(windowCount, monitorCount, userHistory)\n\n  // Rule 1: Low window count -> Pillars (tall, prominent)\n  IF windowCount <= 3 * monitorCount\n    RETURN 'Pillars'\n\n  // Rule 2: Medium window count -> Quads (balanced)\n  IF windowCount <= 4 * monitorCount\n    RETURN 'Quads'\n\n  // Rule 3: High window count -> Extended grid\n  RETURN 'Grid'\n\n  // Rule 4: User override - if user recently switched modes manually,\n  // prefer their choice for 30 minutes\n  IF userHistory.lastManualSwitch > NOW() - 1800000\n    RETURN userHistory.lastSelectedMode\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. State Machine\n\n### 6.1 State Diagram\n\n```\n                              ┌─────────────────┐\n                              │                 │\n                              │      IDLE       │◄────────────────┐\n                              │                 │                 │\n                              └────────┬────────┘                 │\n                                       │                          │\n                            Window position                       │\n                            change detected                       │\n                                       │                          │\n                                       ▼                          │\n                              ┌─────────────────┐                 │\n                              │                 │                 │\n                              │ DRAG_DETECTING  │─────────────────┤\n                              │                 │  Movement < 50px│\n                              └────────┬────────┘  (ignore)       │\n                                       │                          │\n                            Movement >= 50px                      │\n                            Duration >= 300ms                     │\n                                       │                          │\n                                       ▼                          │\n                              ┌─────────────────┐                 │\n                              │                 │                 │\n                              │ DRAG_CONFIRMED  │─────────────────┤\n                              │                 │  Same monitor   │\n                              └────────┬────────┘  (snap to slot) │\n                                       │                          │\n                            Cross-monitor                         │\n                            detected                              │\n                                       │                          │\n                                       ▼                          │\n                              ┌─────────────────┐                 │\n                              │                 │                 │\n                              │ CALCULATING     │                 │\n                              │                 │                 │\n                              └────────┬────────┘                 │\n                                       │                          │\n                           ┌───────────┴───────────┐              │\n                           │                       │              │\n                    Space available         At capacity           │\n                           │                       │              │\n                           ▼                       ▼              │\n                  ┌─────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────┐      │\n                  │                 │    │                 │      │\n                  │ ACCOMMODATING   │    │ BUMP_SELECTING  │      │\n                  │                 │    │                 │      │\n                  └────────┬────────┘    └────────┬────────┘      │\n                           │                       │              │\n                           │              ┌────────┴────────┐     │\n                           │              │                 │     │\n                           │      Found bump        All locked    │\n                           │      candidate          (reject)     │\n                           │              │                 │     │\n                           │              ▼                 │     │\n                           │     ┌─────────────────┐        │     │\n                           │     │                 │        │     │\n                           │     │ BUMP_EXECUTING  │        │     │\n                           │     │                 │        │     │\n                           │     └────────┬────────┘        │     │\n                           │              │                 │     │\n                           └──────────────┴─────────────────┘     │\n                                          │                       │\n                                          ▼                       │\n                              ┌─────────────────┐                 │\n                              │                 │                 │\n                              │   ANIMATING     │                 │\n                              │                 │                 │\n                              └────────┬────────┘                 │\n                                       │                          │\n                            Animation complete                    │\n                            (or timeout 500ms)                    │\n                                       │                          │\n                                       ▼                          │\n                              ┌─────────────────┐                 │\n                              │                 │                 │\n                              │   FINALIZING    │─────────────────┘\n                              │                 │\n                              └─────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 6.2 State Transitions\n\n| Current State | Event | Next State | Action |\n|--------------|-------|------------|--------|\n| IDLE | Position change detected | DRAG_DETECTING | Start tracking movement |\n| DRAG_DETECTING | Movement < 50px for 500ms | IDLE | Ignore (accidental) |\n| DRAG_DETECTING | Movement >= 50px, duration >= 300ms | DRAG_CONFIRMED | Confirm drag intent |\n| DRAG_CONFIRMED | Release on same monitor | IDLE | Snap to nearest slot |\n| DRAG_CONFIRMED | Release on different monitor | CALCULATING | Begin layout calculation |\n| CALCULATING | Target has space | ACCOMMODATING | Simple placement |\n| CALCULATING | Target at capacity | BUMP_SELECTING | Find bump candidate |\n| BUMP_SELECTING | Candidate found | BUMP_EXECUTING | Execute bump |\n| BUMP_SELECTING | All locked | IDLE | Reject drop, return window |\n| ACCOMMODATING | Layout calculated | ANIMATING | Begin smooth transition |\n| BUMP_EXECUTING | Bump complete | ANIMATING | Begin smooth transition |\n| ANIMATING | Animation complete | FINALIZING | Cleanup |\n| FINALIZING | State updated | IDLE | Ready for next action |\n\n### 6.3 Rollback Mechanism\n\nIf user cancels drag (ESC key or returns to original position):\n\n```\nFUNCTION: HandleDragCancel()\n\n  SWITCH currentState\n    CASE DRAG_DETECTING:\n    CASE DRAG_CONFIRMED:\n      // No changes made yet - simply return to idle\n      SET_STATE(IDLE)\n\n    CASE CALCULATING:\n    CASE BUMP_SELECTING:\n      // Calculation in progress - abort and return window\n      RESTORE_WINDOW_TO_ORIGINAL_POSITION(draggedWindow)\n      SET_STATE(IDLE)\n\n    CASE BUMP_EXECUTING:\n    CASE ACCOMMODATING:\n      // Changes being applied - full rollback needed\n      RESTORE_ALL_WINDOWS_TO_SNAPSHOT(preOperationSnapshot)\n      SET_STATE(IDLE)\n\n    CASE ANIMATING:\n      // Animation in progress - stop and rollback\n      STOP_ANIMATION()\n      RESTORE_ALL_WINDOWS_TO_SNAPSHOT(preOperationSnapshot)\n      SET_STATE(IDLE)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Data Structure Definitions\n\n### 7.1 Enhanced matrix_state.json Schema\n\n```json\n{\n  \"layout\": {\n    \"mode\": \"Pillars\",\n    \"maxPillarsPerScreen\": 4,\n    \"gapSize\": 60,\n    \"preferredScreen\": 0,\n    \"windowsOnPrimary\": null,\n\n    \"dynamicAccommodation\": {\n      \"enabled\": true,\n      \"dragThreshold\": 50,\n      \"minDragDuration\": 300,\n      \"animationDuration\": 200,\n      \"quadsOverflowMode\": \"expand\"\n    }\n  },\n\n  \"windowPositions\": {\n    \"Matrix-1\": { \"x\": 60, \"y\": 90, \"width\": 560, \"height\": 930 }\n  },\n\n  \"usageTracking\": {\n    \"Matrix-1\": {\n      \"lastFocusTime\": \"2026-01-19T10:30:00.000Z\",\n      \"totalFocusTime\": 1800000,\n      \"focusHistory\": [],\n      \"isPriorityLocked\": false,\n      \"preferredMonitor\": -1,\n      \"preferredSlot\": -1,\n      \"usageScore\": 0.75,\n      \"lastScoreUpdate\": \"2026-01-19T10:30:00.000Z\"\n    }\n  },\n\n  \"positionPresets\": {\n    \"_autosave\": {\n      \"name\": \"_autosave\",\n      \"createdAt\": \"2026-01-19T10:00:00.000Z\",\n      \"lastUsedAt\": \"2026-01-19T10:30:00.000Z\",\n      \"scope\": \"global\",\n      \"monitorConfiguration\": \"a1b2c3d4\",\n      \"positions\": {}\n    },\n    \"Coding\": {\n      \"name\": \"Coding\",\n      \"createdAt\": \"2026-01-18T08:00:00.000Z\",\n      \"lastUsedAt\": \"2026-01-19T09:00:00.000Z\",\n      \"scope\": \"global\",\n      \"monitorConfiguration\": \"a1b2c3d4\",\n      \"positions\": {}\n    }\n  },\n\n  \"dynamicState\": {\n    \"currentState\": \"IDLE\",\n    \"preOperationSnapshot\": null,\n    \"draggedWindowId\": null,\n    \"sourceMonitor\": null,\n    \"targetMonitor\": null\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 7.2 PowerShell Type Definitions\n\n```powershell\n# WindowUsageData class\nclass WindowUsageData {\n    [string]$WindowId\n    [datetime]$LastFocusTime\n    [int]$TotalFocusTime\n    [array]$FocusHistory\n    [bool]$IsPriorityLocked\n    [int]$PreferredMonitor\n    [int]$PreferredSlot\n    [double]$UsageScore\n    [datetime]$LastScoreUpdate\n}\n\n# PositionPreset class\nclass PositionPreset {\n    [string]$Name\n    [datetime]$CreatedAt\n    [datetime]$LastUsedAt\n    [string]$Scope\n    [string]$MonitorConfiguration\n    [hashtable]$Positions\n}\n\n# DragState enum\nenum DragState {\n    Idle\n    DragDetecting\n    DragConfirmed\n    Calculating\n    BumpSelecting\n    BumpExecuting\n    Accommodating\n    Animating\n    Finalizing\n}\n\n# DynamicLayoutCoordinator class\nclass DynamicLayoutCoordinator {\n    [DragState]$CurrentState\n    [hashtable]$PreOperationSnapshot\n    [string]$DraggedWindowId\n    [int]$SourceMonitor\n    [int]$TargetMonitor\n    [datetime]$DragStartTime\n    [System.Drawing.Point]$DragStartPosition\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Implementation Phases\n\n### Phase 1: Usage Tracking Foundation (2-3 hours)\n- [ ] Add `usageTracking` section to matrix_state.json\n- [ ] Implement `WindowUsageData` class\n- [ ] Add focus tracking via Windows API (GetForegroundWindow polling)\n- [ ] Implement `CalculateUsageScore` function\n- [ ] Add score decay timer\n- [ ] Unit tests for usage scoring\n\n### Phase 2: Drag Detection Engine (3-4 hours)\n- [ ] Implement position monitoring (100ms polling)\n- [ ] Add `DetectDragIntention` function\n- [ ] Cross-monitor detection logic\n- [ ] Integration with existing `Test-WindowDragDetected` function\n- [ ] State machine transitions for IDLE -> DRAG_DETECTING -> DRAG_CONFIRMED\n- [ ] Unit tests for drag detection\n\n### Phase 3: Bump Selection Algorithm (2-3 hours)\n- [ ] Implement `SelectWindowToBump` function\n- [ ] Priority lock functionality (UI toggle)\n- [ ] Integration with usage scores\n- [ ] Fallback to position-based selection\n- [ ] Unit tests for bump selection\n\n### Phase 4: Dynamic Accommodation Core (4-5 hours)\n- [ ] Implement `DynamicAccommodation` algorithm\n- [ ] Pillars mode narrowing logic\n- [ ] Quads mode expansion logic\n- [ ] Cross-monitor window movement\n- [ ] Layout recalculation for affected monitors\n- [ ] State machine transitions for CALCULATING -> ACCOMMODATING/BUMP_SELECTING\n- [ ] Integration tests\n\n### Phase 5: Position Presets System (3-4 hours)\n- [ ] Implement `PositionPreset` class\n- [ ] `SaveCurrentAsPreset` function\n- [ ] `ApplyPreset` function\n- [ ] Monitor configuration validation\n- [ ] Preset adaptation for changed monitors\n- [ ] Auto-save functionality\n- [ ] UI for preset management (new hotkeys)\n\n### Phase 6: Animation and Polish (2-3 hours)\n- [ ] Smooth window movement animation\n- [ ] Rollback mechanism\n- [ ] Error handling and edge cases\n- [ ] Performance optimization (minimize API calls)\n- [ ] Logging integration (US-009 pattern)\n\n### Phase 7: UI Integration (2-3 hours)\n- [ ] Control panel updates (show usage scores, priority locks)\n- [ ] New hotkeys for preset management\n- [ ] Status messages for dynamic operations\n- [ ] Help text updates\n\n---\n\n## 9. Risk Analysis\n\n### 9.1 Technical Risks\n\n| Risk | Impact | Mitigation |\n|------|--------|------------|\n| Focus tracking inaccurate | Medium | Use multiple detection methods (foreground window + click events) |\n| Animation performance | Low | Use SetWindowPos with async flag, limit to 60fps |\n| State corruption | High | Atomic writes, validation on load, automatic repair |\n| Cross-process interference | Medium | Validate window handles before each operation |\n\n### 9.2 User Experience Risks\n\n| Risk | Impact | Mitigation |\n|------|--------|------------|\n| Unexpected bumping | High | Clear visual feedback, undo option, priority locks |\n| Drag detection too sensitive | Medium | Tunable thresholds, user preference for disabling |\n| Presets don't restore correctly | Medium | Monitor validation, graceful adaptation |\n\n---\n\n## 10. Testing Strategy\n\n### 10.1 Unit Tests\n\n```powershell\n# test-dynamic-layout.ps1\n\nDescribe \"Usage Scoring\" {\n    It \"Calculates score correctly for fresh window\" {\n        $score = Calculate-UsageScore -FocusTime 0 -LastFocus (Get-Date)\n        $score | Should -BeLessOrEqual 0.5\n    }\n\n    It \"Boosts score for recently focused window\" {\n        $score = Calculate-UsageScore -FocusTime 3600000 -LastFocus (Get-Date).AddMinutes(-5)\n        $score | Should -BeGreaterOrEqual 0.7\n    }\n\n    It \"Decays score for idle window\" {\n        $score = Calculate-UsageScore -FocusTime 3600000 -LastFocus (Get-Date).AddHours(-2)\n        $score | Should -BeLessOrEqual 0.3\n    }\n}\n\nDescribe \"Drag Detection\" {\n    It \"Ignores small movements\" {\n        $result = Test-DragIntention -StartPos @{X=100;Y=100} -CurrentPos @{X=120;Y=120} -Duration 100\n        $result.Intention | Should -Be 'none'\n    }\n\n    It \"Detects cross-monitor drag\" {\n        $result = Test-DragIntention -StartPos @{X=100;Y=100} -CurrentPos @{X=2000;Y=100} -Duration 500\n        $result.Intention | Should -Be 'cross-monitor'\n    }\n}\n\nDescribe \"Bump Selection\" {\n    It \"Selects lowest score window\" {\n        $windows = @(\n            @{Id=\"Matrix-1\"; Score=0.8; Priority=$false},\n            @{Id=\"Matrix-2\"; Score=0.3; Priority=$false},\n            @{Id=\"Matrix-3\"; Score=0.6; Priority=$false}\n        )\n        $bumped = Select-WindowToBump -Windows $windows\n        $bumped.Id | Should -Be \"Matrix-2\"\n    }\n\n    It \"Skips priority-locked windows\" {\n        $windows = @(\n            @{Id=\"Matrix-1\"; Score=0.1; Priority=$true},\n            @{Id=\"Matrix-2\"; Score=0.8; Priority=$false}\n        )\n        $bumped = Select-WindowToBump -Windows $windows\n        $bumped.Id | Should -Be \"Matrix-2\"\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 10.2 Integration Tests\n\n```powershell\n# test-dynamic-layout-integration.ps1\n\nDescribe \"End-to-End Dynamic Accommodation\" {\n    BeforeAll {\n        # Launch 4 Matrix windows\n        Launch-MatrixWindows -Count 4\n        Start-Sleep -Seconds 2\n    }\n\n    It \"Accommodates 5th window on same monitor\" {\n        # Simulate dragging 5th window to primary monitor\n        $result = Invoke-DynamicAccommodation -TargetMonitor 0 -WindowId \"Matrix-5\"\n        $result.Action | Should -BeIn @('narrow', 'bump')\n\n        # Verify all windows visible\n        $windows = Get-MatrixWindowInfo\n        $windows.Count | Should -Be 5\n    }\n\n    AfterAll {\n        # Close all Matrix windows\n        Get-Process WindowsTerminal | Stop-Process -Force\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 11. Appendix: API Reference\n\n### New Functions to Add to WindowLayoutEngine.ps1\n\n```powershell\n# Usage Tracking\nfunction Get-WindowUsageData { param([string]$WindowId) }\nfunction Update-WindowFocusTime { param([string]$WindowId) }\nfunction Calculate-UsageScore { param([WindowUsageData]$Data) }\nfunction Apply-ScoreDecay { }\n\n# Drag Detection\nfunction Test-DragIntention { param([hashtable]$MovementData) }\nfunction Get-MonitorAtPosition { param([int]$X, [int]$Y) }\nfunction Start-DragMonitoring { }\nfunction Stop-DragMonitoring { }\n\n# Dynamic Accommodation\nfunction Invoke-DynamicAccommodation { param([int]$TargetMonitor, [string]$WindowId) }\nfunction Select-WindowToBump { param([array]$Windows, [string]$ExcludeId) }\nfunction Move-WindowToMonitor { param([IntPtr]$Handle, [int]$MonitorIndex) }\n\n# Position Presets\nfunction Save-PositionPreset { param([string]$Name) }\nfunction Apply-PositionPreset { param([string]$Name) }\nfunction Get-PositionPresets { }\nfunction Remove-PositionPreset { param([string]$Name) }\n\n# State Machine\nfunction Get-DynamicLayoutState { }\nfunction Set-DynamicLayoutState { param([DragState]$State) }\nfunction Invoke-StateTransition { param([string]$Event) }\n```\n\n---\n\n## 12. Conclusion\n\nThis specification provides a complete blueprint for transforming the Matrix Terminal's static window layout system into an intelligent, user-accommodating dynamic layout engine. The key innovations are:\n\n1. **Smart Drag Detection** - Distinguishes intentional drags from accidental movements\n2. **Usage-Based Bumping** - Uses focus tracking to make fair decisions about which windows to move\n3. **Preset System** - Allows users to save and restore window arrangements\n4. **State Machine Architecture** - Ensures reliable, rollback-capable operations\n\nThe implementation is designed to integrate seamlessly with the existing `WindowLayoutEngine.ps1` codebase while adding approximately 800-1000 lines of new functionality across 7 implementation phases.\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":25295,"cache_read_input_tokens":41207,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":25295,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CXHJdwycQ7DCfjhHCN255","type":"assistant","uuid":"c0fd7074-97df-4d88-90ee-1ce11baef1b3","timestamp":"2026-01-19T19:43:11.309Z"}
{"parentUuid":"c0fd7074-97df-4d88-90ee-1ce11baef1b3","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX","sessionId":"7950de6a-373b-44cb-ac45-a9b755daa3bc","version":"2.1.12","gitBranch":"feature/smart-window-management","agentId":"a6b0e92","slug":"reactive-snacking-metcalfe","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PreToolUse","hookName":"PreToolUse:Write","command":"\"C:/Program Files/Python311/python.exe\" ${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/hooks/pretooluse.py"},"toolUseID":"toolu_011SAswPnMyxvW7T8kjVirXp","parentToolUseID":"toolu_011SAswPnMyxvW7T8kjVirXp","uuid":"dc762b82-1293-455f-bb20-240c59ec42f9","timestamp":"2026-01-19T19:43:16.582Z"}
{"parentUuid":"c0fd7074-97df-4d88-90ee-1ce11baef1b3","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX","sessionId":"7950de6a-373b-44cb-ac45-a9b755daa3bc","version":"2.1.12","gitBranch":"feature/smart-window-management","agentId":"a6b0e92","slug":"reactive-snacking-metcalfe","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011SAswPnMyxvW7T8kjVirXp","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: C:\\Users\\ehome\\.claude\\plans\\reactive-snacking-metcalfe-agent-a6b0e92.md"}]},"uuid":"e971ba74-414c-4134-8ae2-fe9a9b4e1cbd","timestamp":"2026-01-19T19:43:26.704Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"create","filePath":"C:\\Users\\ehome\\.claude\\plans\\reactive-snacking-metcalfe-agent-a6b0e92.md","content":"# Dynamic Layout Algorithm Specification\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThis document specifies a **Dynamic Layout Algorithm** for the Matrix Terminal window management system. The algorithm transforms the current static layout engine (Pillars/Quads modes with rigid positioning) into an intelligent, user-accommodating system that:\n\n1. Detects user drag intentions and accommodates window moves\n2. Intelligently bumps least-used windows when monitors reach capacity\n3. Remembers user-customized positions with named presets\n4. Smoothly integrates with existing layout modes\n\n---\n\n## 1. Architecture Overview\n\n### 1.1 Current State Analysis\n\n**Existing Components:**\n- `WindowLayoutEngine.ps1` (1520 lines) - Static layout engine with Pillars/Quads modes\n- `matrix_control.ps1` - Control panel with Shift+L layout cycling\n- `window-registry.json` - Maps window handles to shader files\n- `matrix_state.json` - Persists layout config and window positions\n\n**Current Limitations:**\n- Windows snap back to calculated positions on any re-layout trigger\n- No accommodation for user-dragged windows\n- No usage tracking for intelligent window bumping\n- Position persistence is all-or-nothing (restore entire layout or recalculate)\n\n### 1.2 Proposed Architecture\n\n```\n                                    +------------------------+\n                                    |   Dynamic Layout       |\n                                    |   Coordinator          |\n                                    +------------------------+\n                                              |\n          +-----------------------------------+-----------------------------------+\n          |                   |                   |                   |\n+-----------------+  +------------------+  +------------------+  +------------------+\n| Drag Detection  |  | Usage Tracking   |  | Position         |  | Layout Strategy  |\n| Engine          |  | System           |  | Presets Manager  |  | Adapter          |\n+-----------------+  +------------------+  +------------------+  +------------------+\n| - Movement      |  | - Focus time     |  | - Named presets  |  | - Pillars        |\n|   threshold     |  | - Last active    |  | - Per-monitor    |  | - Quads          |\n| - Cross-monitor |  | - Priority flags |  | - Global sets    |  | - Dynamic        |\n|   detection     |  | - Decay scoring  |  | - Auto-save      |  |   accommodation  |\n+-----------------+  +------------------+  +------------------+  +------------------+\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Dynamic Accommodation Algorithm\n\n### 2.1 Scenario: User Drags Window-3 from Monitor-A to Monitor-B (4 windows)\n\n**Problem Statement:**\nUser drags Window-3 from Monitor-A (which has 2 windows) to Monitor-B (which already has 4 windows at capacity).\n\n**Algorithm Steps:**\n\n```\nALGORITHM: DynamicAccommodation\n\nINPUT:\n  - draggedWindow: Window being dragged\n  - sourceMonitor: Monitor-A (original location)\n  - targetMonitor: Monitor-B (drop destination)\n  - targetCapacity: 4 (max windows per monitor)\n  - allWindows: List of all Matrix windows\n\nOUTPUT:\n  - newLayout: Updated positions for all affected windows\n  - bumpedWindow: Window moved from target to source (if any)\n\nPROCEDURE:\n  1. DETECT_DRAG_INTENTION(draggedWindow, movementData)\n     IF movementDelta < DRAG_THRESHOLD (50px)\n       RETURN null  // Accidental movement, ignore\n\n     IF dragDuration < MIN_DRAG_TIME (300ms)\n       RETURN null  // Too fast, likely unintentional\n\n     targetMonitor = GET_MONITOR_AT_POSITION(releasePosition)\n     IF targetMonitor == sourceMonitor\n       RETURN SNAP_TO_NEAREST_SLOT(draggedWindow, sourceMonitor)\n\n  2. CHECK_TARGET_CAPACITY(targetMonitor)\n     windowsOnTarget = GET_WINDOWS_ON_MONITOR(targetMonitor)\n\n     IF windowsOnTarget.Count < targetCapacity\n       // Space available - simple accommodation\n       ASSIGN_SLOT(draggedWindow, targetMonitor)\n       RECALCULATE_LAYOUT(targetMonitor)\n       RECALCULATE_LAYOUT(sourceMonitor)\n       RETURN newLayout\n\n  3. SELECT_WINDOW_TO_BUMP(windowsOnTarget, draggedWindow)\n     // Use usage scoring to find least-used window\n     candidates = windowsOnTarget.FILTER(w => !w.isPriorityLocked)\n\n     IF candidates.isEmpty\n       RETURN REJECT_DROP()  // All windows are priority-locked\n\n     bumpedWindow = candidates.MIN_BY(w => w.usageScore)\n\n  4. EXECUTE_BUMP(bumpedWindow, sourceMonitor)\n     MOVE_WINDOW(bumpedWindow, sourceMonitor)\n     MOVE_WINDOW(draggedWindow, targetMonitor)\n\n  5. RECALCULATE_LAYOUTS()\n     RECALCULATE_LAYOUT(sourceMonitor)\n     RECALCULATE_LAYOUT(targetMonitor)\n\n  6. UPDATE_TRACKING()\n     UPDATE_POSITION_CACHE(allWindows)\n     UPDATE_USAGE_SCORES(draggedWindow, BOOST)  // User actively used it\n```\n\n### 2.2 Drag Intention Detection\n\n**Criteria for Valid Drag:**\n\n| Criterion | Threshold | Rationale |\n|-----------|-----------|-----------|\n| Movement delta | >= 50px | Below this could be window resize or accidental click |\n| Drag duration | >= 300ms | Quick movements are likely accidental |\n| Cross-monitor | Center crosses boundary | Window center must be on new monitor |\n| Release position | Stable for 100ms | User paused to \"place\" the window |\n\n**Pseudocode:**\n\n```\nFUNCTION: DetectDragIntention\n\nSTATE:\n  - dragStartPosition: {x, y}\n  - dragStartTime: timestamp\n  - dragStartMonitor: MonitorIndex\n  - lastMovementTime: timestamp\n\nPROCEDURE:\n  // Called every 100ms while window is moving\n\n  currentPosition = GET_WINDOW_CENTER(window)\n  currentMonitor = GET_MONITOR_AT_POSITION(currentPosition)\n\n  movementDelta = DISTANCE(dragStartPosition, currentPosition)\n  dragDuration = NOW() - dragStartTime\n\n  // Check if this looks like intentional drag\n  IF movementDelta < 50 AND dragDuration < 500ms\n    RETURN { intention: 'none', confidence: 0.0 }\n\n  IF currentMonitor != dragStartMonitor\n    // Cross-monitor drag detected\n    timeSinceLastMove = NOW() - lastMovementTime\n\n    IF timeSinceLastMove > 100ms\n      // User has paused on new monitor - likely intentional placement\n      RETURN {\n        intention: 'cross-monitor',\n        targetMonitor: currentMonitor,\n        confidence: MIN(1.0, dragDuration / 1000ms)\n      }\n\n  // Same-monitor reposition\n  RETURN {\n    intention: 'reposition',\n    targetMonitor: currentMonitor,\n    confidence: 0.5\n  }\n```\n\n### 2.3 Bump Selection Algorithm\n\n**Criteria for Selecting Window to Bump:**\n\n1. **Priority Lock** - Windows marked as \"priority\" are never bumped\n2. **Usage Score** - Lower score = more likely to be bumped\n3. **Recency** - Windows not focused recently are candidates\n4. **Position** - Prefer bumping windows closest to the source monitor\n\n**Usage Score Calculation:**\n\n```\nFUNCTION: CalculateUsageScore(window)\n\nPARAMETERS:\n  - focusWeight = 0.4      // Weight for focus duration\n  - recencyWeight = 0.3    // Weight for last active time\n  - frequencyWeight = 0.3  // Weight for focus frequency\n\nPROCEDURE:\n  // Focus duration (normalized to 0-1)\n  totalFocusTime = window.focusHistory.SUM(session => session.duration)\n  maxFocusTime = 3600000  // 1 hour cap\n  focusScore = MIN(1.0, totalFocusTime / maxFocusTime)\n\n  // Recency (exponential decay)\n  timeSinceLastFocus = NOW() - window.lastFocusTime\n  decayHalfLife = 1800000  // 30 minutes\n  recencyScore = POW(0.5, timeSinceLastFocus / decayHalfLife)\n\n  // Frequency (focus sessions per hour)\n  sessionsLastHour = window.focusHistory.COUNT(s => s.startTime > NOW() - 3600000)\n  maxSessions = 20  // Cap at 20 sessions per hour\n  frequencyScore = MIN(1.0, sessionsLastHour / maxSessions)\n\n  // Weighted combination\n  usageScore = (focusScore * focusWeight) +\n               (recencyScore * recencyWeight) +\n               (frequencyScore * frequencyWeight)\n\n  // Priority boost (if window is priority-locked)\n  IF window.isPriorityLocked\n    usageScore = 999.0  // Effectively unbumpable\n\n  RETURN usageScore\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Window Usage Tracking System\n\n### 3.1 Data Structure\n\n```\nWindowUsageData = {\n  windowId: string,           // \"Matrix-1\", \"Matrix-2\", etc.\n\n  // Focus tracking\n  lastFocusTime: timestamp,   // Last time window received focus\n  totalFocusTime: number,     // Cumulative focus duration (ms)\n  focusHistory: [             // Rolling window of focus sessions\n    { startTime: timestamp, duration: number }\n  ],\n\n  // User preferences\n  isPriorityLocked: boolean,  // User marked as \"never bump\"\n  preferredMonitor: number,   // User's preferred monitor (-1 = no preference)\n  preferredSlot: number,      // User's preferred slot on monitor (-1 = no preference)\n\n  // Calculated scores (updated on access)\n  usageScore: number,         // 0.0 - 1.0 (higher = more used)\n  lastScoreUpdate: timestamp  // When score was last calculated\n}\n```\n\n### 3.2 Focus Tracking Implementation\n\n```\nFUNCTION: TrackWindowFocus\n\nSTATE:\n  - currentlyFocusedWindow: WindowId | null\n  - focusStartTime: timestamp | null\n\nEVENTS:\n  ON_WINDOW_FOCUS_GAINED(windowId):\n    // End previous focus session\n    IF currentlyFocusedWindow != null\n      EndFocusSession(currentlyFocusedWindow)\n\n    // Start new focus session\n    currentlyFocusedWindow = windowId\n    focusStartTime = NOW()\n\n    // Update last focus time\n    usageData[windowId].lastFocusTime = NOW()\n\n  ON_WINDOW_FOCUS_LOST(windowId):\n    IF currentlyFocusedWindow == windowId\n      EndFocusSession(windowId)\n      currentlyFocusedWindow = null\n\nFUNCTION: EndFocusSession(windowId)\n  duration = NOW() - focusStartTime\n\n  // Add to focus history (keep last 100 sessions)\n  usageData[windowId].focusHistory.PUSH({\n    startTime: focusStartTime,\n    duration: duration\n  })\n  IF usageData[windowId].focusHistory.LENGTH > 100\n    usageData[windowId].focusHistory.SHIFT()\n\n  // Update total focus time\n  usageData[windowId].totalFocusTime += duration\n```\n\n### 3.3 Score Decay Algorithm\n\nUsage scores decay over time to ensure recently-used windows are protected from bumping while idle windows become candidates.\n\n```\nFUNCTION: ApplyScoreDecay\n\nPARAMETERS:\n  - decayInterval = 60000    // Check every 60 seconds\n  - decayFactor = 0.95       // Multiply score by this each interval\n  - minimumScore = 0.1       // Floor to prevent complete decay\n\nPROCEDURE (runs on timer):\n  FOR EACH window IN usageData\n    timeSinceLastUpdate = NOW() - window.lastScoreUpdate\n    decayPeriods = FLOOR(timeSinceLastUpdate / decayInterval)\n\n    IF decayPeriods > 0\n      decayMultiplier = POW(decayFactor, decayPeriods)\n      window.usageScore = MAX(minimumScore, window.usageScore * decayMultiplier)\n      window.lastScoreUpdate = NOW()\n```\n\n### 3.4 Persistence\n\nUsage data persists in `matrix_state.json`:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"layout\": { ... },\n  \"windowPositions\": { ... },\n  \"usageTracking\": {\n    \"Matrix-1\": {\n      \"lastFocusTime\": \"2026-01-19T10:30:00.000Z\",\n      \"totalFocusTime\": 1800000,\n      \"focusHistory\": [\n        { \"startTime\": \"2026-01-19T10:00:00.000Z\", \"duration\": 600000 },\n        { \"startTime\": \"2026-01-19T10:15:00.000Z\", \"duration\": 1200000 }\n      ],\n      \"isPriorityLocked\": false,\n      \"preferredMonitor\": 0,\n      \"preferredSlot\": -1,\n      \"usageScore\": 0.75,\n      \"lastScoreUpdate\": \"2026-01-19T10:30:00.000Z\"\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Custom Snapback Positions (Presets)\n\n### 4.1 Preset Data Structure\n\n```\nPositionPreset = {\n  name: string,              // \"Coding\", \"Monitoring\", \"Presentation\"\n  createdAt: timestamp,\n  lastUsedAt: timestamp,\n\n  // Scope\n  scope: 'global' | 'per-monitor',\n  monitorConfiguration: string,  // Hash of monitor topology for validation\n\n  // Positions (keyed by window ID)\n  positions: {\n    \"Matrix-1\": { x: 60, y: 90, width: 560, height: 930, monitorIndex: 0 },\n    \"Matrix-2\": { x: 680, y: 90, width: 560, height: 930, monitorIndex: 0 },\n    ...\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 4.2 Preset Management API\n\n```\nFUNCTION: SaveCurrentAsPreset(presetName)\n\n  // Capture current window positions\n  windowInfo = Get-MatrixWindowInfo()\n  positions = {}\n\n  FOR EACH window IN windowInfo\n    rect = GET_WINDOW_RECT(window.Handle)\n    monitorIndex = GET_MONITOR_INDEX_FOR_RECT(rect)\n\n    positions[window.ShaderName] = {\n      x: rect.Left,\n      y: rect.Top,\n      width: rect.Right - rect.Left,\n      height: rect.Bottom - rect.Top,\n      monitorIndex: monitorIndex\n    }\n\n  // Generate monitor configuration hash\n  screens = Get-ScreenTopology()\n  monitorConfig = HASH(screens.MAP(s => \"${s.Width}x${s.Height}@${s.Left},${s.Top}\"))\n\n  preset = {\n    name: presetName,\n    createdAt: NOW(),\n    lastUsedAt: NOW(),\n    scope: 'global',\n    monitorConfiguration: monitorConfig,\n    positions: positions\n  }\n\n  // Save to state\n  state.positionPresets[presetName] = preset\n  SAVE_STATE(state)\n\n  RETURN preset\n\n\nFUNCTION: ApplyPreset(presetName)\n\n  preset = state.positionPresets[presetName]\n  IF preset == null\n    RETURN { success: false, error: \"Preset not found\" }\n\n  // Validate monitor configuration matches\n  currentMonitorConfig = GET_CURRENT_MONITOR_CONFIG_HASH()\n  IF preset.monitorConfiguration != currentMonitorConfig\n    // Monitor setup changed - need adaptation\n    RETURN AdaptPresetToCurrentMonitors(preset)\n\n  // Apply positions directly\n  FOR EACH windowId, position IN preset.positions\n    window = FIND_WINDOW_BY_ID(windowId)\n    IF window != null\n      SET_WINDOW_POS(window.Handle, position.x, position.y, position.width, position.height)\n\n  // Update last used time\n  preset.lastUsedAt = NOW()\n  SAVE_STATE(state)\n\n  RETURN { success: true }\n\n\nFUNCTION: AdaptPresetToCurrentMonitors(preset)\n  // Monitor topology changed - intelligently adapt positions\n\n  currentScreens = Get-ScreenTopology()\n\n  FOR EACH windowId, position IN preset.positions\n    window = FIND_WINDOW_BY_ID(windowId)\n    IF window == null\n      CONTINUE\n\n    originalMonitor = position.monitorIndex\n\n    IF originalMonitor < currentScreens.Count\n      // Target monitor still exists - scale position\n      targetScreen = currentScreens[originalMonitor]\n      scaledPosition = SCALE_POSITION_TO_SCREEN(position, targetScreen)\n      SET_WINDOW_POS(window.Handle, scaledPosition)\n    ELSE\n      // Target monitor no longer exists - place on primary\n      primaryScreen = currentScreens[0]\n      scaledPosition = SCALE_POSITION_TO_SCREEN(position, primaryScreen)\n      SET_WINDOW_POS(window.Handle, scaledPosition)\n\n  RETURN { success: true, adapted: true }\n```\n\n### 4.3 Auto-Save Behavior\n\nThe system automatically saves window positions when:\n1. User exits the control panel (ESC key)\n2. User switches layout modes (Shift+L)\n3. After drag accommodation completes\n4. On a timer (every 5 minutes if positions changed)\n\n```\nFUNCTION: SetupAutoSave\n\nSTATE:\n  - lastAutoSave: timestamp\n  - positionsAtLastSave: PositionSnapshot\n  - autoSaveInterval: 300000  // 5 minutes\n\nON_INTERVAL(autoSaveInterval):\n  currentPositions = GET_ALL_WINDOW_POSITIONS()\n\n  IF currentPositions != positionsAtLastSave\n    // Positions changed - auto-save\n    SaveToQuickSnapshot()\n    positionsAtLastSave = currentPositions\n    lastAutoSave = NOW()\n\nFUNCTION: SaveToQuickSnapshot()\n  // Save to special \"_autosave\" preset that can be restored on next session\n  SaveCurrentAsPreset(\"_autosave\")\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Layout Mode Integration\n\n### 5.1 Pillars Mode with Dynamic Accommodation\n\nWhen in Pillars mode, the system can accommodate new windows by:\n\n**Option A: Narrow Existing Columns**\n```\nSCENARIO: 4 pillars on monitor, user drags 5th window to same monitor\n\nBEFORE: [====][====][====][====]  (4 equal-width pillars)\nAFTER:  [===][===][===][===][===] (5 narrower pillars)\n\nCONSTRAINTS:\n  - Minimum pillar width: 300px\n  - If new width < minimum: trigger bump instead\n```\n\n**Option B: Bump Least-Used**\n```\nSCENARIO: 4 pillars at minimum width, user drags 5th window\n\nACTION:\n  1. Identify least-used window via usage scoring\n  2. Bump it to another monitor (or minimize if no monitors available)\n  3. Place dragged window in vacated slot\n  4. Keep pillar widths at minimum\n```\n\n**Implementation:**\n\n```\nFUNCTION: AccommodateInPillarsMode(targetMonitor, draggedWindow)\n\n  currentWindows = GET_WINDOWS_ON_MONITOR(targetMonitor)\n  newCount = currentWindows.Count + 1\n\n  // Calculate new pillar width\n  availableWidth = targetMonitor.Width - (newCount + 1) * GAP_SIZE\n  newPillarWidth = availableWidth / newCount\n\n  IF newPillarWidth >= MIN_PILLAR_WIDTH (300px)\n    // Space available - narrow all pillars\n    RETURN {\n      action: 'narrow',\n      newLayout: CALCULATE_PILLARS_LAYOUT(newCount, targetMonitor)\n    }\n  ELSE\n    // At capacity - need to bump\n    bumpCandidate = SELECT_WINDOW_TO_BUMP(currentWindows, draggedWindow)\n\n    IF bumpCandidate == null\n      // All windows priority-locked\n      RETURN { action: 'reject' }\n\n    RETURN {\n      action: 'bump',\n      bumpWindow: bumpCandidate,\n      newLayout: CALCULATE_PILLARS_LAYOUT(currentWindows.Count, targetMonitor)\n    }\n```\n\n### 5.2 Quads Mode with Dynamic Accommodation\n\nQuads mode has a natural 4-window-per-monitor limit. Accommodation options:\n\n**Option A: Convert to 3x2 Grid**\n```\nSCENARIO: 4 quads on monitor, user drags 5th window\n\nBEFORE:                      AFTER:\n┌─────┐ ┌─────┐             ┌───┐ ┌───┐ ┌───┐\n│  1  │ │  2  │             │ 1 │ │ 2 │ │ 3 │\n└─────┘ └─────┘             └───┘ └───┘ └───┘\n┌─────┐ ┌─────┐             ┌───┐ ┌───┐\n│  3  │ │  4  │             │ 4 │ │ 5 │\n└─────┘ └─────┘             └───┘ └───┘\n\n(Converts from 2x2 to 3x2 grid)\n```\n\n**Option B: Bump to Maintain 2x2**\n```\nSCENARIO: User explicitly wants 2x2 layout\n\nACTION:\n  1. Bump least-used window to another monitor\n  2. Place dragged window in vacated quad\n  3. Maintain 2x2 layout\n```\n\n**Implementation:**\n\n```\nFUNCTION: AccommodateInQuadsMode(targetMonitor, draggedWindow)\n\n  currentWindows = GET_WINDOWS_ON_MONITOR(targetMonitor)\n\n  IF currentWindows.Count < 4\n    // Space available in quad\n    RETURN {\n      action: 'place',\n      newLayout: CALCULATE_QUADS_LAYOUT(currentWindows.Count + 1, targetMonitor)\n    }\n\n  // At 4-window capacity - check user preference\n  layoutConfig = GET_LAYOUT_CONFIG()\n\n  IF layoutConfig.quadsOverflowMode == 'expand'\n    // Convert to 3x2 grid\n    RETURN {\n      action: 'expand',\n      newLayout: CALCULATE_GRID_LAYOUT(5, targetMonitor, cols=3, rows=2)\n    }\n  ELSE\n    // Bump least-used\n    bumpCandidate = SELECT_WINDOW_TO_BUMP(currentWindows, draggedWindow)\n\n    RETURN {\n      action: 'bump',\n      bumpWindow: bumpCandidate,\n      newLayout: CALCULATE_QUADS_LAYOUT(4, targetMonitor)\n    }\n```\n\n### 5.3 Auto Mode Intelligence\n\nAuto mode selects the best layout based on window count and user behavior:\n\n```\nFUNCTION: SelectAutoLayout(windowCount, monitorCount, userHistory)\n\n  // Rule 1: Low window count -> Pillars (tall, prominent)\n  IF windowCount <= 3 * monitorCount\n    RETURN 'Pillars'\n\n  // Rule 2: Medium window count -> Quads (balanced)\n  IF windowCount <= 4 * monitorCount\n    RETURN 'Quads'\n\n  // Rule 3: High window count -> Extended grid\n  RETURN 'Grid'\n\n  // Rule 4: User override - if user recently switched modes manually,\n  // prefer their choice for 30 minutes\n  IF userHistory.lastManualSwitch > NOW() - 1800000\n    RETURN userHistory.lastSelectedMode\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. State Machine\n\n### 6.1 State Diagram\n\n```\n                              ┌─────────────────┐\n                              │                 │\n                              │      IDLE       │◄────────────────┐\n                              │                 │                 │\n                              └────────┬────────┘                 │\n                                       │                          │\n                            Window position                       │\n                            change detected                       │\n                                       │                          │\n                                       ▼                          │\n                              ┌─────────────────┐                 │\n                              │                 │                 │\n                              │ DRAG_DETECTING  │─────────────────┤\n                              │                 │  Movement < 50px│\n                              └────────┬────────┘  (ignore)       │\n                                       │                          │\n                            Movement >= 50px                      │\n                            Duration >= 300ms                     │\n                                       │                          │\n                                       ▼                          │\n                              ┌─────────────────┐                 │\n                              │                 │                 │\n                              │ DRAG_CONFIRMED  │─────────────────┤\n                              │                 │  Same monitor   │\n                              └────────┬────────┘  (snap to slot) │\n                                       │                          │\n                            Cross-monitor                         │\n                            detected                              │\n                                       │                          │\n                                       ▼                          │\n                              ┌─────────────────┐                 │\n                              │                 │                 │\n                              │ CALCULATING     │                 │\n                              │                 │                 │\n                              └────────┬────────┘                 │\n                                       │                          │\n                           ┌───────────┴───────────┐              │\n                           │                       │              │\n                    Space available         At capacity           │\n                           │                       │              │\n                           ▼                       ▼              │\n                  ┌─────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────┐      │\n                  │                 │    │                 │      │\n                  │ ACCOMMODATING   │    │ BUMP_SELECTING  │      │\n                  │                 │    │                 │      │\n                  └────────┬────────┘    └────────┬────────┘      │\n                           │                       │              │\n                           │              ┌────────┴────────┐     │\n                           │              │                 │     │\n                           │      Found bump        All locked    │\n                           │      candidate          (reject)     │\n                           │              │                 │     │\n                           │              ▼                 │     │\n                           │     ┌─────────────────┐        │     │\n                           │     │                 │        │     │\n                           │     │ BUMP_EXECUTING  │        │     │\n                           │     │                 │        │     │\n                           │     └────────┬────────┘        │     │\n                           │              │                 │     │\n                           └──────────────┴─────────────────┘     │\n                                          │                       │\n                                          ▼                       │\n                              ┌─────────────────┐                 │\n                              │                 │                 │\n                              │   ANIMATING     │                 │\n                              │                 │                 │\n                              └────────┬────────┘                 │\n                                       │                          │\n                            Animation complete                    │\n                            (or timeout 500ms)                    │\n                                       │                          │\n                                       ▼                          │\n                              ┌─────────────────┐                 │\n                              │                 │                 │\n                              │   FINALIZING    │─────────────────┘\n                              │                 │\n                              └─────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 6.2 State Transitions\n\n| Current State | Event | Next State | Action |\n|--------------|-------|------------|--------|\n| IDLE | Position change detected | DRAG_DETECTING | Start tracking movement |\n| DRAG_DETECTING | Movement < 50px for 500ms | IDLE | Ignore (accidental) |\n| DRAG_DETECTING | Movement >= 50px, duration >= 300ms | DRAG_CONFIRMED | Confirm drag intent |\n| DRAG_CONFIRMED | Release on same monitor | IDLE | Snap to nearest slot |\n| DRAG_CONFIRMED | Release on different monitor | CALCULATING | Begin layout calculation |\n| CALCULATING | Target has space | ACCOMMODATING | Simple placement |\n| CALCULATING | Target at capacity | BUMP_SELECTING | Find bump candidate |\n| BUMP_SELECTING | Candidate found | BUMP_EXECUTING | Execute bump |\n| BUMP_SELECTING | All locked | IDLE | Reject drop, return window |\n| ACCOMMODATING | Layout calculated | ANIMATING | Begin smooth transition |\n| BUMP_EXECUTING | Bump complete | ANIMATING | Begin smooth transition |\n| ANIMATING | Animation complete | FINALIZING | Cleanup |\n| FINALIZING | State updated | IDLE | Ready for next action |\n\n### 6.3 Rollback Mechanism\n\nIf user cancels drag (ESC key or returns to original position):\n\n```\nFUNCTION: HandleDragCancel()\n\n  SWITCH currentState\n    CASE DRAG_DETECTING:\n    CASE DRAG_CONFIRMED:\n      // No changes made yet - simply return to idle\n      SET_STATE(IDLE)\n\n    CASE CALCULATING:\n    CASE BUMP_SELECTING:\n      // Calculation in progress - abort and return window\n      RESTORE_WINDOW_TO_ORIGINAL_POSITION(draggedWindow)\n      SET_STATE(IDLE)\n\n    CASE BUMP_EXECUTING:\n    CASE ACCOMMODATING:\n      // Changes being applied - full rollback needed\n      RESTORE_ALL_WINDOWS_TO_SNAPSHOT(preOperationSnapshot)\n      SET_STATE(IDLE)\n\n    CASE ANIMATING:\n      // Animation in progress - stop and rollback\n      STOP_ANIMATION()\n      RESTORE_ALL_WINDOWS_TO_SNAPSHOT(preOperationSnapshot)\n      SET_STATE(IDLE)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Data Structure Definitions\n\n### 7.1 Enhanced matrix_state.json Schema\n\n```json\n{\n  \"layout\": {\n    \"mode\": \"Pillars\",\n    \"maxPillarsPerScreen\": 4,\n    \"gapSize\": 60,\n    \"preferredScreen\": 0,\n    \"windowsOnPrimary\": null,\n\n    \"dynamicAccommodation\": {\n      \"enabled\": true,\n      \"dragThreshold\": 50,\n      \"minDragDuration\": 300,\n      \"animationDuration\": 200,\n      \"quadsOverflowMode\": \"expand\"\n    }\n  },\n\n  \"windowPositions\": {\n    \"Matrix-1\": { \"x\": 60, \"y\": 90, \"width\": 560, \"height\": 930 }\n  },\n\n  \"usageTracking\": {\n    \"Matrix-1\": {\n      \"lastFocusTime\": \"2026-01-19T10:30:00.000Z\",\n      \"totalFocusTime\": 1800000,\n      \"focusHistory\": [],\n      \"isPriorityLocked\": false,\n      \"preferredMonitor\": -1,\n      \"preferredSlot\": -1,\n      \"usageScore\": 0.75,\n      \"lastScoreUpdate\": \"2026-01-19T10:30:00.000Z\"\n    }\n  },\n\n  \"positionPresets\": {\n    \"_autosave\": {\n      \"name\": \"_autosave\",\n      \"createdAt\": \"2026-01-19T10:00:00.000Z\",\n      \"lastUsedAt\": \"2026-01-19T10:30:00.000Z\",\n      \"scope\": \"global\",\n      \"monitorConfiguration\": \"a1b2c3d4\",\n      \"positions\": {}\n    },\n    \"Coding\": {\n      \"name\": \"Coding\",\n      \"createdAt\": \"2026-01-18T08:00:00.000Z\",\n      \"lastUsedAt\": \"2026-01-19T09:00:00.000Z\",\n      \"scope\": \"global\",\n      \"monitorConfiguration\": \"a1b2c3d4\",\n      \"positions\": {}\n    }\n  },\n\n  \"dynamicState\": {\n    \"currentState\": \"IDLE\",\n    \"preOperationSnapshot\": null,\n    \"draggedWindowId\": null,\n    \"sourceMonitor\": null,\n    \"targetMonitor\": null\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 7.2 PowerShell Type Definitions\n\n```powershell\n# WindowUsageData class\nclass WindowUsageData {\n    [string]$WindowId\n    [datetime]$LastFocusTime\n    [int]$TotalFocusTime\n    [array]$FocusHistory\n    [bool]$IsPriorityLocked\n    [int]$PreferredMonitor\n    [int]$PreferredSlot\n    [double]$UsageScore\n    [datetime]$LastScoreUpdate\n}\n\n# PositionPreset class\nclass PositionPreset {\n    [string]$Name\n    [datetime]$CreatedAt\n    [datetime]$LastUsedAt\n    [string]$Scope\n    [string]$MonitorConfiguration\n    [hashtable]$Positions\n}\n\n# DragState enum\nenum DragState {\n    Idle\n    DragDetecting\n    DragConfirmed\n    Calculating\n    BumpSelecting\n    BumpExecuting\n    Accommodating\n    Animating\n    Finalizing\n}\n\n# DynamicLayoutCoordinator class\nclass DynamicLayoutCoordinator {\n    [DragState]$CurrentState\n    [hashtable]$PreOperationSnapshot\n    [string]$DraggedWindowId\n    [int]$SourceMonitor\n    [int]$TargetMonitor\n    [datetime]$DragStartTime\n    [System.Drawing.Point]$DragStartPosition\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Implementation Phases\n\n### Phase 1: Usage Tracking Foundation (2-3 hours)\n- [ ] Add `usageTracking` section to matrix_state.json\n- [ ] Implement `WindowUsageData` class\n- [ ] Add focus tracking via Windows API (GetForegroundWindow polling)\n- [ ] Implement `CalculateUsageScore` function\n- [ ] Add score decay timer\n- [ ] Unit tests for usage scoring\n\n### Phase 2: Drag Detection Engine (3-4 hours)\n- [ ] Implement position monitoring (100ms polling)\n- [ ] Add `DetectDragIntention` function\n- [ ] Cross-monitor detection logic\n- [ ] Integration with existing `Test-WindowDragDetected` function\n- [ ] State machine transitions for IDLE -> DRAG_DETECTING -> DRAG_CONFIRMED\n- [ ] Unit tests for drag detection\n\n### Phase 3: Bump Selection Algorithm (2-3 hours)\n- [ ] Implement `SelectWindowToBump` function\n- [ ] Priority lock functionality (UI toggle)\n- [ ] Integration with usage scores\n- [ ] Fallback to position-based selection\n- [ ] Unit tests for bump selection\n\n### Phase 4: Dynamic Accommodation Core (4-5 hours)\n- [ ] Implement `DynamicAccommodation` algorithm\n- [ ] Pillars mode narrowing logic\n- [ ] Quads mode expansion logic\n- [ ] Cross-monitor window movement\n- [ ] Layout recalculation for affected monitors\n- [ ] State machine transitions for CALCULATING -> ACCOMMODATING/BUMP_SELECTING\n- [ ] Integration tests\n\n### Phase 5: Position Presets System (3-4 hours)\n- [ ] Implement `PositionPreset` class\n- [ ] `SaveCurrentAsPreset` function\n- [ ] `ApplyPreset` function\n- [ ] Monitor configuration validation\n- [ ] Preset adaptation for changed monitors\n- [ ] Auto-save functionality\n- [ ] UI for preset management (new hotkeys)\n\n### Phase 6: Animation and Polish (2-3 hours)\n- [ ] Smooth window movement animation\n- [ ] Rollback mechanism\n- [ ] Error handling and edge cases\n- [ ] Performance optimization (minimize API calls)\n- [ ] Logging integration (US-009 pattern)\n\n### Phase 7: UI Integration (2-3 hours)\n- [ ] Control panel updates (show usage scores, priority locks)\n- [ ] New hotkeys for preset management\n- [ ] Status messages for dynamic operations\n- [ ] Help text updates\n\n---\n\n## 9. Risk Analysis\n\n### 9.1 Technical Risks\n\n| Risk | Impact | Mitigation |\n|------|--------|------------|\n| Focus tracking inaccurate | Medium | Use multiple detection methods (foreground window + click events) |\n| Animation performance | Low | Use SetWindowPos with async flag, limit to 60fps |\n| State corruption | High | Atomic writes, validation on load, automatic repair |\n| Cross-process interference | Medium | Validate window handles before each operation |\n\n### 9.2 User Experience Risks\n\n| Risk | Impact | Mitigation |\n|------|--------|------------|\n| Unexpected bumping | High | Clear visual feedback, undo option, priority locks |\n| Drag detection too sensitive | Medium | Tunable thresholds, user preference for disabling |\n| Presets don't restore correctly | Medium | Monitor validation, graceful adaptation |\n\n---\n\n## 10. Testing Strategy\n\n### 10.1 Unit Tests\n\n```powershell\n# test-dynamic-layout.ps1\n\nDescribe \"Usage Scoring\" {\n    It \"Calculates score correctly for fresh window\" {\n        $score = Calculate-UsageScore -FocusTime 0 -LastFocus (Get-Date)\n        $score | Should -BeLessOrEqual 0.5\n    }\n\n    It \"Boosts score for recently focused window\" {\n        $score = Calculate-UsageScore -FocusTime 3600000 -LastFocus (Get-Date).AddMinutes(-5)\n        $score | Should -BeGreaterOrEqual 0.7\n    }\n\n    It \"Decays score for idle window\" {\n        $score = Calculate-UsageScore -FocusTime 3600000 -LastFocus (Get-Date).AddHours(-2)\n        $score | Should -BeLessOrEqual 0.3\n    }\n}\n\nDescribe \"Drag Detection\" {\n    It \"Ignores small movements\" {\n        $result = Test-DragIntention -StartPos @{X=100;Y=100} -CurrentPos @{X=120;Y=120} -Duration 100\n        $result.Intention | Should -Be 'none'\n    }\n\n    It \"Detects cross-monitor drag\" {\n        $result = Test-DragIntention -StartPos @{X=100;Y=100} -CurrentPos @{X=2000;Y=100} -Duration 500\n        $result.Intention | Should -Be 'cross-monitor'\n    }\n}\n\nDescribe \"Bump Selection\" {\n    It \"Selects lowest score window\" {\n        $windows = @(\n            @{Id=\"Matrix-1\"; Score=0.8; Priority=$false},\n            @{Id=\"Matrix-2\"; Score=0.3; Priority=$false},\n            @{Id=\"Matrix-3\"; Score=0.6; Priority=$false}\n        )\n        $bumped = Select-WindowToBump -Windows $windows\n        $bumped.Id | Should -Be \"Matrix-2\"\n    }\n\n    It \"Skips priority-locked windows\" {\n        $windows = @(\n            @{Id=\"Matrix-1\"; Score=0.1; Priority=$true},\n            @{Id=\"Matrix-2\"; Score=0.8; Priority=$false}\n        )\n        $bumped = Select-WindowToBump -Windows $windows\n        $bumped.Id | Should -Be \"Matrix-2\"\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 10.2 Integration Tests\n\n```powershell\n# test-dynamic-layout-integration.ps1\n\nDescribe \"End-to-End Dynamic Accommodation\" {\n    BeforeAll {\n        # Launch 4 Matrix windows\n        Launch-MatrixWindows -Count 4\n        Start-Sleep -Seconds 2\n    }\n\n    It \"Accommodates 5th window on same monitor\" {\n        # Simulate dragging 5th window to primary monitor\n        $result = Invoke-DynamicAccommodation -TargetMonitor 0 -WindowId \"Matrix-5\"\n        $result.Action | Should -BeIn @('narrow', 'bump')\n\n        # Verify all windows visible\n        $windows = Get-MatrixWindowInfo\n        $windows.Count | Should -Be 5\n    }\n\n    AfterAll {\n        # Close all Matrix windows\n        Get-Process WindowsTerminal | Stop-Process -Force\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 11. Appendix: API Reference\n\n### New Functions to Add to WindowLayoutEngine.ps1\n\n```powershell\n# Usage Tracking\nfunction Get-WindowUsageData { param([string]$WindowId) }\nfunction Update-WindowFocusTime { param([string]$WindowId) }\nfunction Calculate-UsageScore { param([WindowUsageData]$Data) }\nfunction Apply-ScoreDecay { }\n\n# Drag Detection\nfunction Test-DragIntention { param([hashtable]$MovementData) }\nfunction Get-MonitorAtPosition { param([int]$X, [int]$Y) }\nfunction Start-DragMonitoring { }\nfunction Stop-DragMonitoring { }\n\n# Dynamic Accommodation\nfunction Invoke-DynamicAccommodation { param([int]$TargetMonitor, [string]$WindowId) }\nfunction Select-WindowToBump { param([array]$Windows, [string]$ExcludeId) }\nfunction Move-WindowToMonitor { param([IntPtr]$Handle, [int]$MonitorIndex) }\n\n# Position Presets\nfunction Save-PositionPreset { param([string]$Name) }\nfunction Apply-PositionPreset { param([string]$Name) }\nfunction Get-PositionPresets { }\nfunction Remove-PositionPreset { param([string]$Name) }\n\n# State Machine\nfunction Get-DynamicLayoutState { }\nfunction Set-DynamicLayoutState { param([DragState]$State) }\nfunction Invoke-StateTransition { param([string]$Event) }\n```\n\n---\n\n## 12. Conclusion\n\nThis specification provides a complete blueprint for transforming the Matrix Terminal's static window layout system into an intelligent, user-accommodating dynamic layout engine. The key innovations are:\n\n1. **Smart Drag Detection** - Distinguishes intentional drags from accidental movements\n2. **Usage-Based Bumping** - Uses focus tracking to make fair decisions about which windows to move\n3. **Preset System** - Allows users to save and restore window arrangements\n4. **State Machine Architecture** - Ensures reliable, rollback-capable operations\n\nThe implementation is designed to integrate seamlessly with the existing `WindowLayoutEngine.ps1` codebase while adding approximately 800-1000 lines of new functionality across 7 implementation phases.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c0fd7074-97df-4d88-90ee-1ce11baef1b3"}
