{"parentUuid":"ae8fd21f-6583-425d-be6a-0660a58ad3c6","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"C:\\Users\\ehome\\Documents\\MATRIX","sessionId":"7950de6a-373b-44cb-ac45-a9b755daa3bc","version":"2.1.12","gitBranch":"feature/smart-window-management","agentId":"a6b0e92","slug":"reactive-snacking-metcalfe","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_017iHvaQ1vnNoV9utVbFnsHz","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I have created a comprehensive algorithm specification document for the Dynamic Layout Algorithm. Let me provide you with a summary of the key design decisions and architecture.\n\n---\n\n## Dynamic Layout Algorithm - Architecture Summary\n\n### Core Problem Addressed\n\nThe current `WindowLayoutEngine.ps1` (1520 lines) implements static Pillars and Quads layouts that rigidly position windows. When users drag windows, they snap back to calculated positions. The new Dynamic Layout Algorithm transforms this into an intelligent, user-accommodating system.\n\n### Architecture Components\n\n**1. Dynamic Layout Coordinator (Orchestrator)**\nManages the overall state machine and coordinates between subsystems:\n- `DragState` enum: IDLE -> DRAG_DETECTING -> DRAG_CONFIRMED -> CALCULATING -> ACCOMMODATING/BUMP_SELECTING -> ANIMATING -> FINALIZING\n- Handles rollback if user cancels mid-operation\n\n**2. Drag Detection Engine**\nDistinguishes intentional drags from accidental movements:\n- Movement threshold: 50px minimum\n- Duration threshold: 300ms minimum  \n- Cross-monitor detection: Window center must cross monitor boundary\n- Release stability: User pauses 100ms to \"place\" window\n\n**3. Usage Tracking System**\nTracks window usage for intelligent bump decisions:\n- Focus duration history (rolling window of last 100 sessions)\n- Recency scoring with exponential decay (30-minute half-life)\n- Priority locks for windows that should never be bumped\n- Persisted to `matrix_state.json`\n\n**4. Bump Selection Algorithm**\nWhen target monitor is at capacity:\n```\nusageScore = (focusScore * 0.4) + (recencyScore * 0.3) + (frequencyScore * 0.3)\nbumpedWindow = candidates.MIN_BY(w => w.usageScore)\n```\nPriority-locked windows get score of 999.0 (effectively unbumpable).\n\n**5. Position Presets Manager**\nNamed position configurations (e.g., \"Coding\", \"Monitoring\"):\n- Per-preset monitor topology validation\n- Automatic adaptation when monitors change\n- Auto-save every 5 minutes and on exit\n\n### Key Algorithm: Dynamic Accommodation\n\n```\nUser drags Window-3 from Monitor-A (2 windows) to Monitor-B (4 windows at capacity)\n\n1. DETECT_DRAG_INTENTION\n   - Verify movement > 50px, duration > 300ms\n   - Confirm cross-monitor (window center on Monitor-B)\n\n2. CHECK_TARGET_CAPACITY\n   - Monitor-B has 4 windows (at capacity for Quads mode)\n\n3. SELECT_WINDOW_TO_BUMP\n   - Filter out priority-locked windows\n   - Select lowest usageScore window on Monitor-B\n   - Example: Matrix-2 has score 0.3 (idle for 45 minutes)\n\n4. EXECUTE_BUMP\n   - Move Matrix-2 to Monitor-A (where Window-3 came from)\n   - Move Window-3 to Monitor-B (user's intended destination)\n\n5. RECALCULATE_LAYOUTS\n   - Monitor-A: Recalculate Pillars/Quads for new window set\n   - Monitor-B: Recalculate with Window-3 replacing Matrix-2\n\n6. ANIMATE_TRANSITION\n   - Smooth 200ms animation for all affected windows\n   - Maintain rollback snapshot until animation completes\n```\n\n### Layout Mode Integration\n\n**Pillars Mode Accommodation:**\n- Option A: Narrow existing columns (if new width >= 300px minimum)\n- Option B: Bump least-used window (if at minimum width)\n\n**Quads Mode Accommodation:**\n- Option A: Expand to 3x2 grid (configurable via `quadsOverflowMode`)\n- Option B: Bump least-used to maintain 2x2 layout\n\n### Data Structures\n\nThe enhanced `matrix_state.json` adds:\n```json\n{\n  \"layout\": {\n    \"dynamicAccommodation\": {\n      \"enabled\": true,\n      \"dragThreshold\": 50,\n      \"minDragDuration\": 300,\n      \"animationDuration\": 200\n    }\n  },\n  \"usageTracking\": {\n    \"Matrix-1\": {\n      \"lastFocusTime\": \"...\",\n      \"usageScore\": 0.75,\n      \"isPriorityLocked\": false\n    }\n  },\n  \"positionPresets\": {\n    \"Coding\": { \"positions\": {...} }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### Implementation Estimate\n\n| Phase | Description | Hours |\n|-------|-------------|-------|\n| 1 | Usage Tracking Foundation | 2-3 |\n| 2 | Drag Detection Engine | 3-4 |\n| 3 | Bump Selection Algorithm | 2-3 |\n| 4 | Dynamic Accommodation Core | 4-5 |\n| 5 | Position Presets System | 3-4 |\n| 6 | Animation and Polish | 2-3 |\n| 7 | UI Integration | 2-3 |\n| **Total** | | **18-25** |\n\n### Files to Modify/Create\n\n1. **WindowLayoutEngine.ps1** - Add ~800-1000 lines for new subsystems\n2. **matrix_state.json** - Extended schema for usage tracking and presets\n3. **matrix_control.ps1** - New hotkeys for presets, priority lock toggle\n4. **matrix_monitor.ps1** (new) - Background process for continuous drag monitoring\n\n---\n\nThe complete specification with pseudocode, state diagrams, and data structure definitions is in the plan file at:\n`C:\\Users\\ehome\\.claude\\plans\\reactive-snacking-metcalfe-agent-a6b0e92.md`"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":11130,"cache_read_input_tokens":66502,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":11130,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CXHJsLFRi98ZSQRPdGcgL","type":"assistant","uuid":"05b697b9-ce2b-49f8-8f59-7f74b966499d","timestamp":"2026-01-19T19:43:58.292Z"}
